# Wiktionary Maltese - Letter B_v1 (Refined)

Total Entries: 839

## abbasta (v-abbasta)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to suffice, to be enough
    **MT**: Kien biżżejjed jew kellu ammont jew livell kbir u sodisfaċenti f'ammont.
- **Alternative Forms**: bbasta
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-ikel kien jabbasta għall-mistednin kollha li attendew.
    EN: The food was enough for all the guests who attended.

---

## b' (prep-b)
- **POS**: preposition
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: with (chiefly an ingredient, means, concomitant, quality)
    **MT**: Prepożizzjoni użata biex tindika mezz, ingredjent jew kumpanija, ma'.
  - **EN**: times, (multiplied) by
    **MT**: Użat fil-multiplikazzjoni jew dimensjonijiet, ippultiplikat b'.
- **Alternative Forms**: bi, imb
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Saq ir-rota b'veloċità kbira fit-triq.
    EN: He rode the bicycle with great speed on the road.

---

## b'xejn (adj-bxejn)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: free of charge
    **MT**: Mingħajr l-ebda ħlas, miżata jew kost finanzjarju, b'rigal.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ingħataw il-kotba b'xejn lill-istudenti fl-iskola.
    EN: The books were given free of charge to students in school.

---

## b'ħerqa (adv-bħerqa)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: eagerly
    **MT**: B'entużjażmu kbir, xewqa u impenn sħiħ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-istudenti semgħu b'ħerqa l-aħbar it-tajba.
    EN: The students listened eagerly to the good news.

---

## babun (n-babun)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: baboon
    **MT**: Speċi ta' xadin kbir b'denb b'saħħtu li jgħix fuq l-art fl-Afrika u fil-Lvant Nofsani.
- **Alternative Forms**: babwin
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-babun kien qed jitla' fuq il-blat biex ifittex l-ikel.
    EN: The baboon was climbing the rocks to search for food.

---

## babwin (n-babwin)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: baboon
    **MT**: Xadin kbir tal-pjanuri u tal-blat fil-kontinent Afrikan, babun.
- **Alternative Forms**: babun
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rajna babwin f'riżerva naturali matul il-vjaġġ.
    EN: We saw a baboon in a natural reserve during the trip.

## bad (v-bad)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to lay (eggs)
    **MT**: Ħarġet u poġġiet l-bajd minn ġisimha (b'referenza għat-tjur u rettili).
- **Alternative Forms**: bied
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tiġieġa bajdet żewġ bajdiet fil-nest tal-qafas.
    EN: The hen laid two eggs in the coop nest.

---

## badbad (v-badbad)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-d-b-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to be lecherous
    **MT**: Aġixxa b'mod lussurjuż jew wera xewqa eċċessiva lejn nies oħra.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien magħrufa li jbadbad fit-toroq u ġie mwissi.
    EN: He was known to be lecherous in the streets and was warned.

## badessa (n-badessa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: abbess
    **MT**: Superjura jew kap ta' kunvent tan-nuni, abadissa.
- **Alternative Forms**: abbatissa
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-badessa laqgħet id-delegazzjoni uffiċjali fil-monasteru.
    EN: The abbess welcomed the official delegation to the monastery.

## bafer (n-bafer)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: buffer (someone or something that buffs)
    **MT**: Persuna jew apparat użat biex inaqqas l-impatt jew il-konfrontazzjoni ikkawżata minn ħabta.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Użaw sistema ta' bafer biex jipproteġu n-netwerk kompjuterizzat.
    EN: They used a buffer system to protect the computerized network.

## bagalja (n-bagalja)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a piece of luggage, baggage
    **MT**: Oġġett b'qafas iebes jew artab użat biex iġorr il-ħwejjeġ fil-vjaġġ.
  - **EN**: a suitcase (specifically)
    **MT**: Basket jew kaxxa spetsifika magħluqa bil-ħafna spazju ghal viżti.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ppakka l-bagalja bil-ħwejjeġ qabel it-titjira.
    EN: He packed the suitcase with clothes before the flight.

## bagoll (n-bagoll)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: large piece of luggage or baggage
    **MT**: Kaxxa kbira jew bagalja tqila tal-injam jew metall għall-vjaġġi.
  - **EN**: the trunk of a vehicle
    **MT**: Il-parti ta' wara ta' karozza fejn jitpoġġew il-basktijiet u l-oġġetti.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ppoġġa l-basktijiet fil-bagoll tal-karozza.
    EN: He put the bags in the trunk of the car.

## bagular (n-bagular)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: European nettle tree (Celtis australis)
    **MT**: Sġra kbira tal-weraq b'injam iebes li tikber fil-Mediterran (Celtis australis).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Is-siġra tal-bagular kienet tagħti dell kbir fil-pjazza.
    EN: The European nettle tree provided great shade in the square.

## bagħad (v-bagħad)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-għ-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to hate
    **MT**: Krahajt minn qiegħ qalbek lil xi ħadd jew xi ħaġa mingħajr tolleranza.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien ibagħad il-gideb u l-mewġ fir-relazzjonijiet.
    EN: He hated lies and deceit in relationships.

---

## bagħal (n-bagħal)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-għ-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: mule
    **MT**: Annimal ibridu magħmul mit-tqabbil ta' ħmar ma' dabra.
  - **EN**: bastard
    **MT**: Tifel imwieled minn ġenituri mhux miżżewġin uffiċjalment.
  - **EN**: hardhearted person
    **MT**: Persuna b'qalb iebsa u mingħajr sentimenti.
  - **EN**: fruitless sprout
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal fruitless sprout.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bagħal kien iġorr il-merkanzija fit-triq iebsa tal-muntanja.
    EN: The mule carried the cargo on the tough mountain road.

---

## bagħal (v-bagħal)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-għ-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to bear mules
    **MT**: Welled bagħal jew tkabbar u żewweġ razez differenti biex tirriżulta f'ibridu.
  - **EN**: to interbreed
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to interbreed.
  - **EN**: to be fruitless
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to be fruitless.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-fermer bagħal l-annimali tiegħu fir-razzett.
    EN: The farmer bred mules on his farm.

---

## bagħar (n-bagħar)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-għ-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dung
    **MT**: Skart u ħmieġ solidu tal-annimali u tal-bhejjem fit-trobbija.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Użaw il-bagħar biex jiffertilizzaw il-ħamrija tal-għalqa.
    EN: They used the dung to fertilise the field soil.

---

## bagħat (v-bagħat)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-għ-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to send
    **MT**: Trasmettejt jew esegwiet tluq ta' ittra, messaġġ jew oġġett lil xi ħadd.
  - **EN**: to text
    **MT**: Bdejt timbotta jew ttrasmetti messaġġ elettroniku fuq il-mowbajl.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bagħat ittra uffiċjali lill-amministrazzjoni tal-iskola.
    EN: He sent an official letter to the school administration.

---

## bagħbas (v-bagħbas)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-għ-b-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to finger
    **MT**: Messet, irranġajt jew tbagħbset b'mod mhux awtorizzat ma' apparat jew oġġett.
  - **EN**: to tamper, to meddle
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to tamper, to meddle.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bagħbas mas-sokra sakemm fetħet mingħajr mhi meħtieġa l-imsaħħan.
    EN: He tampered with the lock until it opened.

---

## bagħbus (n-bagħbus)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-għ-b-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a person who has the habit of touching things or people when talking (Is there an English equivalent to this definition?)
    **MT**: Persuna li għandha l-vizzju li tmiss l-oġġetti jew lin-nies waqt li tkun qed titkellem.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien bagħbus magħruf u n-nies kienu jneħħu l-oġġetti minn quddiemu.
    EN: He was a habitual toucher and people moved items from before him.

---

## bagħdam (n-bagħdam)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: harrier, especially the western marsh harrier
    **MT**: Għasfur predatorju bil-ġwienaħ twal li jinsab fil-widien u fil-bwar (bagħdan).
- **Alternative Forms**: bagħdan
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bagħdam kien itir fuq il-wied biex jaqbad il-ġriewi.
    EN: The harrier was flying over the valley to catch mice.

---

## bagħdan (n-bagħdan)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: harrier, especially the western marsh harrier
    **MT**: Għasfur predatorju kbir tal-familja Accipitridae li jinsab fi bwar u salini.
- **Alternative Forms**: bagħdam
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Osservaw il-bagħdan jikkaċċja fil-pjanuri miftuħa.
    EN: They observed the harrier hunting in open plains.

---

## bagħdan abjad (n-bagħdan-abjad)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: pallid harrier (Circus macrourus)
    **MT**: Speċi ta' bagħdan bir-rix griż u abjad fuq in-naħa ta' taħt (Circus macrourus).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bagħdan abjad huwa rari li jidher matul il-passa f'Malta.
    EN: The pallid harrier is rare to see during migration in Malta.

---

## bagħdan aħmar (n-bagħdan-aħmar)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: western marsh harrier (Circus aeruginosus)
    **MT**: Speċi ta' bagħdan kbir bil-kulur kannella u aħmar li jgħix fis-salini (Circus aeruginosus).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bagħdan aħmar jinsab spiss fil-widien mimlija ilma.
    EN: The western marsh harrier is often found in flooded valleys.

---

## bagħdan griż (n-bagħdan-griż)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Montagu's harrier (Circus pygargus)
    **MT**: Speċi ta' bagħdan irqiq bir-rix griż li jbejjet f'għelieqi miftuħa (Circus pygargus).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bagħdan griż għadda minn fuq il-gżejjer fir-rebbiegħa.
    EN: Montagu's harrier passed over the islands in spring.

---

## bagħdan ikħal (n-bagħdan-ikħal)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: hen harrier (Circus cyaneus)
    **MT**: Speċi ta' bagħdan li għandu r-rix tal-ġwienaħ bil-kulur ikħal u griż (Circus cyaneus).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-ornitologi rreġistraw il-bagħdan ikħal fil-lixtar.
    EN: Ornithologists recorded the hen harrier in the sanctuary.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-għ-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: female equivalent of bagħal
    **MT**: Forma femminili ta' bagħal, bagħla femminili magħmula minn ħmar u dabra.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bagħla kienet mħarrġa sew biex timxi mal-passaġġi rqaq.
    EN: The female mule was well trained to walk narrow paths.

- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: unirrigated (field)
    **MT**: Li ma jinstabx tisqija artifiċjali u jiddependi biss fuq ix-xita fil-biedja.
- **Usage Examples**:

    EN: Unirrigated land produces fewer crops in drought years.

    EN: He called the neighbour an abusive person after he caused trouble.

- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-għ-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dunglike
    **MT**: Li għandu r-riħa jew il-proprjetajiet u s-simili tal-bagħar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ħamrija kellha kulur u riħa bagħrija wara l-fertilizzant.

## bagħtar (v-bagħtar)

- **Root**: b-għ-t-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to walk through mud

  - **EN**: to till wet land

- **Usage Examples**:

    EN: He walked through the road mud to reach home.

## bahrad (v-bahrad)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-h-r-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to enjoy oneself boisterously
    **MT**: Ħadt gost u ddevertajt b'mod storbjuż u mmarkat b'ħafna daħk u ferħ.

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bahrad u ddeverta ma meħtieġ mas-sħabu kollha fil-festa.
    EN: He enjoyed himself boisterously with all his friends at the party.

## bahraġ (v-bahraġ)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to enjoy oneself boisterously
    **MT**: Għext divertiment storbjuż u ferrieħi, bahrad.
- **Alternative Forms**: bahrad
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bahraġ fit-toroq sal-ewwel sigħat tal-sodda.

- **Definitions**:

- **Definitions**:

## bajja (n-bajja)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm ħafna għawwiema fil-bajja r-ramlija.

- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-j-d

- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-j-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to whitewash, to paint a wall
    **MT**: Pitter jew diljak il-ħitan b'saff ta' ġir jew żebgħa bajda.

  - **EN**: to polish

- **Definitions**:

## bajtar (n-bajtar)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: figs
    **MT**: Frott mill-isbaħ li jagħtu s-siġar tal-tini jew tal-bajtar tax-xewk.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Indian fig, Opuntia ficus-indica
    **MT**: Frott tipiku tal-Mediterran b'xewk minn pjanta kaktus (Opuntia ficus-indica).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bajtar tax-xewk huwa magħruf għat-togħma ħelwa tiegħu fis-sajf.
    EN: Prickly pear is known for its sweet taste in summer.

## bakar (v-bakar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-k-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to anticipate
    **MT**: Għamilt xi ħaġa qabel il-ħin mistenni jew ħriġt l-ewwel frott tal-istaġun.
  - **EN**: to produce the firstfruits
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to produce the firstfruits.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-frott bakar fl-għelieqi minħabba s-sħana kmieni.
    EN: The fruit ripened early in the fields due to early heat.

## bakkar (v-bakkar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-k-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to get up early
    **MT**: Qomt jew bdiet azzjoni kmieni ħafna filgħodu qabel l-oħrajn.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bakkar kmieni biex jasal l-ewwel fil-post tax-xogħol.
    EN: He got up early to arrive first at the workplace.

## bala’ (v-bala)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to swallow
    **MT**: Tgħappejt jew bla' ikel u xorb mill-sider għall-istonku.
  - **EN**: to be gullible
    **MT**: Pjanti jew temmejt storja jew gideb mingħajr ma tivverifikaha.
- **Alternative Forms**: bela'
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bala' l-pillola b'tazza ilma sħiħa.
    EN: He swallowed the pill with a full glass of water.

---

## balbal (v-balbal)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-b-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to gibber, to babble
    **MT**: Tatkellimt b'mod storbjuż, mgħaġġel u bla sens li ma jinftiehemx.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Beda jbalbal u jitkellem bla waqien meta kien eċċitat.
    EN: He started babbling and speaking endlessly when excited.

---

## balbuljata (n-balbuljata)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: A traditional Maltese dish featuring scrambled eggs cooked with tomatoes and onions.
    **MT**: Platt tradizzjonali Malti magħmul minn bajd imħawwad b'tadam u basal imsajjar.
  - **EN**: mess
    **MT**: Sitwazzjoni mħawda u mimlija konfużjoni.
- **Alternative Forms**: barbuljata
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Sajru balbuljata bil-tadam friska għall-kolazzjon.
    EN: They cooked scrambled eggs with fresh tomatoes for breakfast.

---

## balja (v-balja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to oscillate
    **MT**: Tbandalt u ċċaqlaqt bil-mod u mhux b'mod sod.
  - **EN**: to be unsteady
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to be unsteady.
- **Alternative Forms**: bbalija
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dgħajsa bdiet tbalja mar-riħ qawwi fil-port.
    EN: The boat began to oscillate with the strong wind in the port.

---

## balka (v-balka)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-k-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to abate, to calm down
    **MT**: Għaddew u kkalmaw il-mewġ jew ir-riħ u t-temp wara maltempata.
- **Alternative Forms**: bbalka
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baħar balka wara li kien hemm maltempata kbira.
    EN: The sea abated after a major storm.

---

## ballas (v-ballas)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to mess about
    **MT**: Aġixxa u mmanipulajt affarijiet bla skop u f'konfużjoni.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Beda jballas fil-ħanut u rranġa ftit oġġetti.
    EN: He started messing about in the shop and arranged a few items.

---

## ballat (v-ballat)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to beat down, to pound
    **MT**: Dakka u ċċattjajt it-tajn jew l-art b'għodda jew saqajn biex tagħmilha iebsa.
  - **EN**: to flatten
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to flatten.
  - **EN**: to pack by beating down
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to pack by beating down.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ballat l-art tal-għalqa biex jagħmilha solida.
    EN: He pounded the field ground to make it solid.

---

## balla’ (v-balla)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make or cause to swallow
    **MT**: Għamilt jew għent lil xi ħadd jibla' l-ikel jew l-ilma.
  - **EN**: to make someone believe anything
    **MT**: Għamilt lil xi ħadd jemmen dikjarazzjoni falza b'gideb.
- **Alternative Forms**: bella'
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Balla' l-mediċina lit-tifel bil-mod.
    EN: He made the child swallow the medicine slowly.

---

## ballottra (n-ballottra)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: weasel
    **MT**: Annimal żgħir predatorju bil-ġisem twil u r-rix kannella li jikkaċċja r-riżorsi (weasel).
  - **EN**: rockling
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal rockling.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ballottra ħarbet fil-ħajt tal-sejjieħ hekk kif qrabna.
    EN: The weasel fled into the rubble wall as we approached.

---

## ballun (n-ballun)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: ball
    **MT**: Oġġett tond u mimli arja użat fil-logħob jew sports bħall-futbol.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tfal kienu jilagħbu bil-ballun fil-pjazza.
    EN: The children were playing with the ball in the square.

---

## ballun baraxx (n-ballun-baraxx)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: hot-air balloon
    **MT**: Ballun kbir li jttir bl-arja sħuna u l-gass f'sema miftuħ.
  - **EN**: a fat person
    **MT**: Użat metaforikament għal persuna bl-ożità kbira u fula.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rajna ballun baraxx jttir fuq il-widien.
    EN: We saw a hot-air balloon flying over the valleys.

---

## ballut (n-ballut)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: holm oak, Quercus ilex
    **MT**: Siġra kbira u b'saħħitha tal-Mediterran bil-frott tal-ġandar u injam iebes (Quercus ilex).
  - **EN**: oak
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal oak.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-masġar tal-ballut f'Malta huwa protett bil-liġi.
    EN: The holm oak grove in Malta is protected by law.

---

## ballut Ingliż (n-ballut-ingliż)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: English oak, Quercus robur
    **MT**: Speċi ta' sġra tal-ballut komuni fit-tramuntana tal-Ewropa (Quercus robur).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ballut Ingliż jikber sa tul kbir u jgħix għal sekli.
    EN: English oak grows to a great height and lives for centuries.

---

## balzmina (n-balzmina)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: synonym of balzmuwa: balsam apple (Momordica balsamina)
    **MT**: Pjanta bil-fjur aħmar u frott żgħir użata fil-mediċina, balzmuwa (Momordica balsamina).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-balzmina tikber spontanjament f'ċerti għelieqi.
    EN: Balsam apple grows spontaneously in certain fields.

---

## balzmu (n-balzmu)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: balm, balsam
    **MT**: Sustanza magħmula miż-żjut b'riħa tajba użata biex ittaffi l-uġigħ.
  - **EN**: comfort
    **MT**: Kumdità u serħan morali u spiritwali mogħti lil persuna nkwetata.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-kliem tiegħu kienu balzmu għal qalbha mweġġa'.
    EN: His words were a balm to her hurt heart.

---

## balzmuwa (n-balzmuwa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: balsam apple (Momordica balsamina)
    **MT**: Pjanta li tagħti frott żgħir b'propjetajiet ta' kura medika, balzmina.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Użaw l-estratt tal-balzmuwa fil-preparazzjoni tradizzjonali.
    EN: They used balsam apple extract in the traditional preparation.

---

## bambin (n-bambin)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: baby, toddler
    **MT**: Tarbija jew tifel żgħir f'età bix-xewqa li jimxi.
  - **EN**: almost exclusively used referring to Baby Jesus and therefore often capitalized as il-Bambin (Ġesù)
    **MT**: Statwa ta' Ġesù Tarbija użata matul il-festa tal-Milied (il-Bambin).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ppoġġew il-Bambin fil-grotta tal-presepju.
    EN: They placed the Baby Jesus in the nativity cave.

---

## bambinella (n-bambinella)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Maltese June pear (Pyrus communis var. bambinella)
    **MT**: Varijetà ta' lanġas żgħir u ħelu Malti li jsir f'Ġunju (Pyrus communis var. bambinella).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Xtraw basket bambinella friska mis-suq lokali.
    EN: They bought a basket of fresh June pears from the local market.

---

## bamboċċ (n-bamboċċ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: simpleton
    **MT**: Persuna faċli u b'nuqqas ta meħtieġ ta' moħħ li kulħadd jidħak biha.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Tittrattahx bħala bamboċċ għax huwa bniedem intelliġenti.
    EN: Don't treat him as a simpleton because he is an intelligent person.

---

## bamboċċata (n-bamboċċata)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: childish behaviour
    **MT**: Aġir jew azzjoni childish u bla sens magħmula minn persuna adult.
- **Alternative Forms**: bambuċċata
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienet bamboċċata sħiħa li ħalqet konfużjoni bla bżonn.
    EN: It was complete childish behaviour that created unnecessary confusion.

---

## bambuċċata (n-bambuċċata)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: childish behaviour
    **MT**: Aġir childish u bla sens, bamboċċata.
- **Alternative Forms**: bamboċċata
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rrifjuta li jidħol f'dik il-bambuċċata tagħhom.
    EN: He refused to take part in that childish behaviour of theirs.

---

## bamer (n-bamer)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bummer, loafer
    **MT**: Persuna li ma tħobbx taħdem u li tqatta' ż-żmien ma tagħmel xejn.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien bamer li kien joqgħod sedut fil-pjazza l-ġurnata kollha.
    EN: He was a loafer who sat in the square all day.

---

## banana (n-banana)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: banana (fruit)
    **MT**: Frott twil bil-qoxra isfar u laħam artab u ħelu mill-pajjiżi tropikali.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kiel banana bħala parti mill-kolazzjon tiegħu.
    EN: He ate a banana as part of his breakfast.

---

## banda (n-banda)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: side
    **MT**: Parti jew naħa waħda minn oġġett jew territorju.
  - **EN**: band
    **MT**: Soċjetà jew każin mużikali b'orkestra li ddaqq fil-festi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-banda tar-raħal daqqet marċi ferrieħi fit-toroq.
    EN: The village band played cheerful marches in the streets.

---

## bandal (v-bandal)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-n-d-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to swing, rock
    **MT**: Tbandalt jew ċaqlaqt oġġett 'il quddiem u lura b'mod regolari.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bandal it-tifel fuq il meħtieġ tal-bandla fil-park.
    EN: He rocked the child on the swing in the park.

---

## bandiera (n-bandiera)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: flag
    **MT**: Pezz drapp b'kuluri u disinni li jirrappreżentaw pajjiż jew soċjetà.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għollew il-bandiera nazzjonali fuq il-palazz tal-gvern.
    EN: They raised the national flag on the government palace.

---

## bandla (n-bandla)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-n-d-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: swing (hanging seat)
    **MT**: Sedil imdendel b'rabtiet għat-tfal biex jitbandlu.
  - **EN**: pendulum
    **MT**: Oġġett tqil f'arloġġ li jiċċaqlaq minn naħa għal oħra.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tfal ferħu meta ppoġġew bandla ġdida fil-ġnien.
    EN: The children were happy when they placed a new swing in the garden.

---

## banju (n-banju)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bath
    **MT**: L-att jew il-proċess ta' ħasil tal-ġisem kollu fl-ilma.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħa banju sħun biex jistrieħ wara ġurnata xogħol.
    EN: He took a hot bath to relax after a day's work.

---

## banjumarija (n-banjumarija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bain-marie
    **MT**: Metodu ta' tisjir billi tpoġġi r-reċipjent mal-ikel f'kassol fl-ilma jaħraq.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħallat iċ-ċikkulata fil-banjumarija biex ma tinħaraqx.
    EN: He melted the chocolate in a bain-marie so it wouldn't burn.

---

## bank (n-bank)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bank (financial building or institution)
    **MT**: Istituzzjoni finanzjarja fejn in-nies jiddepożitaw u jissellfu l-flus.
  - **EN**: bank (an underwater area of higher elevation, a sandbank)
    **MT**: Sedil twil tal-injam fil-knejjes jew fi ġonna.
  - **EN**: bench
    **MT**: Mejda twila użata mix-xogħol tal-artjani u l-imħallfin.
  - **EN**: counter (table or board on which business is transacted)
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal counter.
  - **EN**: worktable
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal worktable.
  - **EN**: judge 's seat
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal judge 's seat.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Depożita l-flus fil-bank lokali.
    EN: He deposited the money in the local bank.

---

## bankett (n-bankett)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: banquet
    **MT**: Ikla kbira u solenni organizzata fil-festa b'ħafna mistednin.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Attendew għall-bankett uffiċjali li sar fil-palazz.
    EN: They attended the official banquet held at the palace.

---

## bankina (n-bankina)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: curb or pavement
    **MT**: Mogħdija mgħollija mal-ġenb tat-triq iddisinjata għall-persuni li jimxu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Mxejtu fuq il-bankina biex ikunu siguri mit-traffiku.
    EN: They walked on the pavement to stay safe from traffic.

---

## bannas (v-bannas)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-n-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to assert repeatedly
    **MT**: Saħaqt u spjegajt kontinwament stqarrija jew talba b'persistenza.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bannas dwar l-importanza tar-regola l-ġdida.
    EN: He asserted repeatedly the importance of the new rule.

---

## bannat (v-bannat)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-n-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to produce a stem
    **MT**: Ħarġet u żviluppat zokk jew sistema ta' friegħi bil-weraq fil-pjanti.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Is-siġra bannat b'weraq ġodda fir-rebbiegħa.
    EN: The tree produced a stem with new leaves in spring.

---

## bans (adv-bans)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Root**: b-n-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: truly, undeniably
    **MT**: B'mod ċert, bla dubju u veritier f'kull użu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bans kienet l-aħjar deċiżjoni li seta' jieħu.
    EN: It was truly the best decision he could take.

---

## baqa' (v-baqa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-q-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to remain, to stay
    **MT**: Zammejt u ma ċċaqlaqtx minn post jew stat partikolari.
  - **EN**: to agree
    **MT**: Għamilt ftehim jew kunsens ma' persuna oħra.
  - **EN**: Used with a following active participle to convey a continuous action
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' Used with a following active participle to convey a continuous action.
  - **EN**: Used with an intransitive verb in the imperfect to convey a habitual or repeated action
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' Used with an intransitive verb in the imperfect to convey a habitual or repeated action.
  - **EN**: Used with a transitive verb in the imperfect to convey a continuous action
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' Used with a transitive verb in the imperfect to convey a continuous action.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baqa' d-dar minħabba l-maltempati barra.
    EN: He stayed home because of the storms outside.

---

## baqat (v-baqat)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-q-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to coagulate, curdle (milk)
    **MT**: Sar solidu u koagula bħall-ħalib jew id-demm wara żmien.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ħalib baqat minħabba s-sħana li kien hemm fil-kċina.
    EN: The milk curdled due to the heat in the kitchen.

---

## baqbaq (v-baqbaq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-q-b-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to bubble up, to boil violently
    **MT**: Faqqa' u ħareġ bużżieqa f'likwidu li jinsab jagħli b'saħħa.
  - **EN**: to coo, gurgle
    **MT**: Ħareġ ilħna artab bħall-ħamiem jew tkellem bla waqfien.
  - **EN**: to chatter
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to chatter.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-ilma beda jbaqbaq f'kassol fuq in-nar.
    EN: The water started to bubble up in the pot on the fire.

---

## baqgħa (n-baqgħa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-q-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of baqa’: stay agreement
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' baqa', l-att li tibqa' u tagħmel ftehim f'post.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baqgħa tiegħu fil-lukanda kienet estiża b'ġimgħa.
    EN: His stay at the hotel was extended by a week.

---

## baqla (n-baqla)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-q-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: eczema
    **MT**: Marda fuq il-ġilda li tikkawża aħmar u ħakk kbir (ekżema).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-użu ta' krema speċjali għen biex tfejjaq il-baqla.
    EN: The use of a special cream helped cure the eczema.

---

## baqq (n-baqq)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-q-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bugs, bedbugs (several or the species as such)
    **MT**: Insetti żgħar u ċatti li jgħixu fis-saqqijiet u l-għamara u jduqu d-demm (bedbugs).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Tratta d-dar biex jelimina l-baqq kollu mill-kmamar.
    EN: He treated the house to eliminate all bedbugs from the rooms.

---

## baqqa' (v-baqqa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-q-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make (someone) stay
    **MT**: Zammejt lil xi ħadd b'saħħa jew għamiltu jibqa' f'post u ma jitlaqx.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baqqa' lill-mistednin sad-tmiem tal-festa.
    EN: He made the guests stay until the end of the party.

---

## baqqan (v-baqqan)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-q-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to work with a pickaxe
    **MT**: Ħdemt u ċaqlaqt il-blat jew il-ħamrija billi użajt baqqun.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baqqan il-blat iebes biex jiftaħ kanal għall-ilma.
    EN: He worked with a pickaxe on the hard rock to open a water channel.

---

## baqqat (v-baqqat)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-q-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to coagulate, curdle (milk)
    **MT**: Għamilt sustanza tagħqad flimkien f'massa solida, baqat.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baqqat il-ħalib biex jagħmel il-ġbejniet tradizzjonali.
    EN: He curdled the milk to make traditional cheeselets.

---

## baqqiegħa (n-baqqiegħa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: large field
    **MT**: Għalqa kbira u miftuħa jew post fit-tajn fejn jinġabar l-ilma tax-xita.
  - **EN**: bottom of a duct or any place where water is collected (Is there an English equivalent to this definition?)
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal bottom of a duct or any place where water is collected.
  - **EN**: marshland
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal marshland.
- **Alternative Forms**: boqqiegħa
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-ilma tax-xita inġabar fil-baqqiegħa tal-wied.
    EN: Rainwater gathered in the marshland of the valley.

---

## baqqun (n-baqqun)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-q-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: pickaxe
    **MT**: Għodda tqila tal-ħadid bil-ponta użata biex taqsam il-blat u l-ħamrija iebsa.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Uża l-baqqun biex ikisser il-blat fil-pedament.
    EN: He used the pickaxe to break the rock in the foundation.

---

## baqra (n-baqra)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: cow
    **MT**: Annimal kbir u domestiiku fir-razzett li jagħti l-ħalib u l-laħam.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baqra kienet qed tiekol il-ħaxix fil-mergħa.
    EN: The cow was eating grass in the pasture.

---

## baqra tal-baħar (n-baqra-tal-baħar)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sea cow
    **MT**: Mammiferu kbir u rari tal-baħar li jiekol pjanti tal-ilma.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għaddiesa rajt baqra tal-baħar tgħum fil-fond.
    EN: The divers saw a sea cow swimming in the deep.

---

## barad (v-barad)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-r-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to polish, to file
    **MT**: Lixxajt jew saħħabt oġġett tal-metall jew injam billi użajt lima.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Barad it-truf tal-pjanċa biex ma jibqgħux jaqtgħu.
    EN: He filed the edges of the plate so they wouldn't be sharp.

---

## baram (v-baram)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-r-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to twist
    **MT**: Għawweġ jew dawwar oġġett ma' xulxin f'forma ta' korda jew sigarett.
  - **EN**: to roll
    **MT**: Għamilt pjan minn taħt biex tfixkel jew tqarraq lil xi ħadd.
  - **EN**: to pilfer
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to pilfer.
  - **EN**: to deceive, to outwit
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to deceive, to outwit.
  - **EN**: to occur to [with lil]
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to occur to [with lil].
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baram il-ħabel sew biex iżid is-saħħa tiegħu.
    EN: He twisted the rope tightly to increase its strength.

---

## barax (v-barax)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-r-x
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to scrape
    **MT**: Ħakkjajt u skrappjajt wiċċ ta' oġġett billi użajt strument iebes.
  - **EN**: to scratch
    **MT**: Stealt jew ksibt il-premji kollha faċilment fil-logħob.
  - **EN**: to win everything easily (in a gamble, for example)
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to win everything easily.
  - **EN**: to steal, to pilfer
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to steal, to pilfer.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Barax iż-żebgħa l-antika mill-bieb tal-injam.
    EN: He scraped the old paint from the wooden door.

---

## barbuljata (n-barbuljata)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: A traditional Maltese dish featuring scrambled eggs cooked with tomatoes and onions.
    **MT**: Platt tradizzjonali ta' bajd imħawwad b'tadam, balbuljata.
  - **EN**: mess
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal mess.
- **Alternative Forms**: balbuljata
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Preżentaw barbuljata sħuna mal-ħobż tal-Malti.
    EN: They served hot scrambled eggs with tomatoes with Maltese bread.

---

## bard (n-bard)
- **POS**: noun
- **Root**: b-r-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: chill
    **MT**: Bard jew kesħa kbira fl-arja u fit-temp li tagħmel il-kesħa tiżdied.
  - **EN**: chill weather
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal chill weather.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm bard kbir filgħaxija matul ix-xitwa.
    EN: There was a great chill in the evening during winter.

---

## bardan (adj-bardan)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-r-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: cold
    **MT**: Li iħoss il-kesħa u l-bard ma' ġismu kollu, kiesaħ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tifel kien bardan wara li ħareġ mit-pixxina.
    EN: The child was cold after coming out of the pool.

---

## bardgħa (n-bardgħa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: packsaddle
    **MT**: Sedil jew saqqu padded imqiegħed fuq dahar il-ħmar jew il-bagħal biex iġorr it-tagħbija.
- **Alternative Forms**: berdgħa
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ppoġġew il-bardgħa fuq id-dahar tal-ħmar qabel il-vjaġġ.
    EN: They placed the packsaddle on the donkey's back before the journey.

---

## barju (n-barju)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: barium (chemical element)
    **MT**: Element kimiku metalliku bil-kulur fidda b'simbolu Ba u numru atomiku 56.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-barju jintuża fl-analiżi medika tar-raġġi X.
    EN: Barium is used in X-ray medical analysis.

---

## barka (n-barka)
- **POS**: noun
- **Root**: b-r-k
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: blessing
    **MT**: Favur, protezzjoni u qdusija divina mogħtija lil persuna jew proġett.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-qassis ta l-barka lill-familja l-ġdida.
    EN: The priest gave the blessing to the new family.

---

## barka t'Alla (n-barka-talla)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: great abundance
    **MT**: Kwantità enormi, faċilità u reżultati pożittivi ipprovduti minn Alla.
- **Alternative Forms**: barket Alla
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għandhom barka t'Alla fl-għalqa tagħhom bil-frott.
    EN: They have great abundance in their field with fruit.

---

## barket Alla (n-barket-alla)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: great abundance
    **MT**: Kwantità kbira u provvista tajba, barka t'Alla.
- **Alternative Forms**: barka t'Alla
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm barket Alla fl-ikla tal-Milied.
    EN: There was great abundance at the Christmas meal.

---

## barma (n-barma)
- **POS**: noun
- **Root**: b-r-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: twist, rolling
    **MT**: Rotazzjoni u dawra ma' xulxin f'forma ta' korda jew oħsu fl-injam.
  - **EN**: stoutness
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal stoutness.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-arloġġ kellu barma li kienet jeħtieġ li tiġi mdawra.
    EN: The clock had a twist that needed to be turned.

---

## barmil (n-barmil)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bucket
    **MT**: Vettura u reċipjent tond bil-manku użat biex iġorr l-ilma u likwidi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Mla l-barmil b'ilma nadif mill-viti tal-ġnien.
    EN: He filled the bucket with clean water from the garden tap.

---

## barnaż (v-barnaż)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-r-n-ż
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to wear a hood, a cowl
    **MT**: Għatta jew libes b'kaxxola jew barnuż fuq rasu u spalltu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Barnaż rasu meta bdiet tagħmel ix-xita qawwija.
    EN: He wore a hood on his head when heavy rain started.

---

## barnuż (n-barnuż)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-r-n-ż
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: hood, cowl
    **MT**: Parti minn kowt jew ilbies li tintlibes fuq ir-ras biex tipproteġi mil-xita jew il-kesħa.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għolla l-barnuż biex ma jxarrabx xagħru.
    EN: He raised the hood so his hair wouldn't get wet.

---

## barnuża (n-barnuża)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-r-n-ż
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of barnuż
    **MT**: Barnuż żgħir li jkopri r-ras tat-tfal jew partijiet minn kostum.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tarbija kellha barnuża roża mas-suf tagħha.
    EN: The baby had a pink hood with her woollen coat.

---

## barr (n-barr)
- **POS**: noun
- **Root**: b-r-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: wilderness
    **MT**: Art selvaġġa u miftuħa użata biex tindika postijiet u r-raba' selvaġġ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħarbu fil-barr miftuħ matul il-gwerra.
    EN: They fled into the open wilderness during the war.

---

## barr (v-barr)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-r-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to coo (make a coo sound)
    **MT**: Rrodd u ħareġ l-ilħna artab u ripetuttiv (b'referenza għall-ħamiem).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ħamiema bdiet tibarr fuq il-gallarija.
    EN: The pigeon began to coo on the balcony.

---

## barra (adv-barra)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Root**: b-r-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: out, outside, outdoors
    **MT**: Fl-ispazju miftuħ u barra mill-bini jew mid-dar.
  - **EN**: abroad
    **MT**: F'pajjiżi oħra lil hinn minn pajjiżek (barra minn Malta).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Marru jilagħbu barra minħabba li s-sema kien xemxi.
    EN: They went to play outside because the sky was sunny.

---

## barra (prep-barra)
- **POS**: preposition
- **Root**: b-r-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: outside (of)
    **MT**: Lil hinn mill-konfini ta' post jew bini.
  - **EN**: except
    **MT**: Minkejja u minbarra persuni jew oġġetti oħra, minbarra.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kulħadd kien preżenti barra Marija li kienet marida.
    EN: Everyone was present except Mary who was ill.

---

## barrada (n-barrada)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-r-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: file (cutting or smoothing tool)
    **MT**: Għodda b'uċuħ li jaqtgħu u jaħku użata biex tillixxa l-metall, lima.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Uża l-barrada biex lixxa l-pjanċa tal-azzar.
    EN: He used the file to smooth the steel plate.

---

## barrani (adj-barrani)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: foreign
    **MT**: Li jappartjeni jew ikun magħmul minn pajjiż ieħor u mhux nattiv.
  - **EN**: foreigner
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' foreigner.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien jitkellem lingwa barranija bil-flwenza sħiħa.
    EN: He spoke a foreign language fluently.

---

## barraxa (n-barraxa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-r-x
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: scraper
    **MT**: Għodda b'xafra iebsa użata biex tobrox żebgħa antika jew tajn mill-ħitan.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Naddaf il-faċċata billi uża l-barraxa.
    EN: He cleaned the façade using the scraper.

---

## barri (adj-barri)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-r-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: rural, wild
    **MT**: Li ikun selvaġġ u mhux mgħallem jew li jappartjeni għar-raba' u bwar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Pjanta barrija li tikber f'bosta bwar selvaġġi.
    EN: A wild plant that grows in many wild pastures.

---

## barri (n-barri)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-r-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bull
    **MT**: Annimal maskili kbir u b'saħħtu tal-bhejjem bil-qrun.
  - **EN**: strong
    **MT**: Persuna b'forza fiżika u b'saħħa kbira fir-rispett.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-barri kien qed jimxi fil-mergħa man-nagħaġ.
    EN: The bull was walking in the pasture with the sheep.

---

## barriera (n-barriera)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: quarry
    **MT**: Post miftuħ ta' skavjar fejn jinqatgħu l-ġebel tal-bini (franka jew qallieq).
  - **EN**: barrier
    **MT**: Struttura jew xkiel li ma jippermettix passaġġ liberu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Jaħdem fil-barriera billi jaqta' l-blat tal-bini.
    EN: He works in the quarry cutting building stone.

---

## barważ (v-barważ)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to cause hernia
    **MT**: Ħoloq konfużjoni, ħawwad il-fehma ta' xi ħadd jew wettaq xogħol bil-mod u bla arti.
  - **EN**: to perform clumsy work
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to perform clumsy work.
  - **EN**: to talk confusedly
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to talk confusedly.
  - **EN**: to discombobulate
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to discombobulate.
- **Alternative Forms**: bażwar
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Barważ fil-kitba u ħalla ħafna żbalji uffiċjali.
    EN: He performed clumsy work in writing and left many errors.

---

## barxa (n-barxa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-r-x
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a scratch
    **MT**: Marka jew ferita qasira fuq il-ġilda ikkawżata minn ħakk ma' oġġett iebes.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kellu barxa żgħira fuq idu wara li waqa'.
    EN: He had a small scratch on his hand after falling.

---

## barxa fl-istonku (n-barxa-fl-istonku)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: peptic ulcer
    **MT**: Ferita jew ulċera fil-ħajt tal-istonku li tikkawża uġigħ (ulċera peptika).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tabib ikkonferma li kellu barxa fl-istonku minħabba l-istress.
    EN: The doctor confirmed he had a peptic ulcer due to stress.

---

## barżakka (n-barżakka)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: knapsack, haversack
    **MT**: Basket tat-tila jew tal-ġilda użat mis-suldat jew minn nies fuq id-dahar minflok bagalja.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ppakka l-ikel kollu fil-barżakka qabel mar jimxi fil-kampanja.
    EN: He packed all the food in the knapsack before walking in the countryside.

---

## basal (n-basal)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: onion (as a mass or species)
    **MT**: Ħaxix f'forma ta' boċċi b'togħma u riħa qawwija użat fit-tisjir u fit-taħwir.
  - **EN**: several onions
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal several onions.
  - **EN**: bulbs
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal bulbs.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Qatta' l-basal fir-rqieq qabel ma qelieh mal-tadam.
    EN: He chopped the onions finely before frying them with tomatoes.

---

## basar (v-basar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-s-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to foresee, foretell, predict
    **MT**: Mbassar jew għaraf minn qabel dwar avvenimenti li se jseħħu fil-futur.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-profeta basar id-diffikultajiet li kienu se jiġu.
    EN: The prophet foretold the difficulties that were to come.

---

## basi (n-basi)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: base
    **MT**: Il-parti ta' taħt u l-istruttura li fuqha tinbena deċiżjoni, bini jew argument, bażi.
  - **EN**: basis
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal basis.
  - **EN**: base
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal base.
- **Alternative Forms**: bażi
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Stabbilew il-basi ta' pjan finanzjarju solidu.
    EN: They established the basis of a solid financial plan.

---

## basra (n-basra)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-s-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a prediction
    **MT**: Tbassir jew stqarrija uffiċjali dwar avvenimenti futuri, predizzjoni.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-basra tiegħu rriżultat li kienet eżatta ħafna.
    EN: His prediction turned out to be very accurate.

---

## bass (n-bass)
- **POS**: noun
- **Root**: b-s-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: farting
    **MT**: Ħoss u skart tal-gass mill-imsaren jew vettura kbira tat-trasport (bax/bus).
  - **EN**: bus
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal bus.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ir-raġel bagħat bass matul it-triq.
    EN: The man farted loudly along the way.

---

## bass (v-bass)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-s-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to fart loudly
    **MT**: Ħarġet gass b'ħoss mill-imsaren jew għamilt għajjat b'disprezz lill-persuna.
  - **EN**: to boo (someone)
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to boo.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien jagħmel l-aġir bla mġiba u bass fil-pubbliku.
    EN: He acted improperly and farted in public.

---

## bassa (n-bassa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Root**: b-s-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a fart
    **MT**: L-att jew l-emissjoni ta' gass mill-imsaren.
  - **EN**: a meddler
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal a meddler.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għamlet bassa li stordiet lill-persuni fil-kamra.
    EN: She emitted a fart that stunned people in the room.

---

## bast (intj-bast)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: so long as, provided
    **MT**: Esclamazzjoni u konġunzjoni li tfisser 'sakemm' jew 'basta'.
  - **EN**: in spite of
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala in spite of.
- **Alternative Forms**: basta
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Tista' tiġi, bast tatti b'mod xieraq.
    EN: You may come, provided you behave appropriately.

---

## bast (prep-bast)
- **POS**: preposition
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: so long as, provided
    **MT**: Sakemm, basta, bil-kundizzjoni li.
  - **EN**: in spite of
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala in spite of.
- **Alternative Forms**: basta
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Nisimgħuk, bast tkun onest magħna.
    EN: We will listen to you, provided you are honest with us.

---

## basta (intj-basta)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: enough, stop, shut up
    **MT**: Esclamazzjoni użata biex twaqqaf aġir jew titlob is-skiet, biżżejjed!
- **Alternative Forms**: bast
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għajjat 'basta!' biex itemm d-diskussjoni dik il-ħin.
    EN: He shouted 'enough!' to terminate the discussion at that moment.

---

## basta (prep-basta)
- **POS**: preposition
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: so long as, provided
    **MT**: Bil-kundizzjoni li jew sakemm, bast.
  - **EN**: in spite of
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala in spite of.
- **Alternative Forms**: bast
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Se ngħinek, basta tikkollabora miegħi.
    EN: I will help you, provided you collaborate with me.

---

## bastun (n-bastun)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: cane, stick, staff
    **MT**: Bastun tal-injam użat bħala appoġġ mal-mixi minn nies anzjani jew morda.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ir-raġel kien jimxi bl-għajnuna ta' bastun tal-injam.
    EN: The man walked with the help of a wooden stick.

---

## bastun ta' San Ġuzepp (n-bastun-ta-san-ġuzepp)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: hollyhock, Alcea rosea
    **MT**: Pjanta b'fjur kulurit u zokk twil ħafna fil-ġonna (Alcea rosea).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bastun ta' San Ġużepp ikun mimli fjur fir-rebbiegħa.
    EN: Hollyhock is full of flowers in spring.

---

## bata (v-bata)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-t-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to suffer
    **MT**: Għex fit-tbatija u ħass uġigħ fiżiku jew morali f'marda jew fil-faqar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bata ħafna matul il-marda tiegħu fil-sodda.
    EN: He suffered a lot during his illness in bed.

---

## batal (v-batal)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-t-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become vacant, empty
    **MT**: Sparixxa l-użu ta meħtieġ jew sar vojt kif ukoll ħadt mistrieħ mix-xogħol.
  - **EN**: to take a break
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to take a break.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Batal mix-xogħol għal siegħa biex jiekol.
    EN: He took a break from work for an hour to eat.

---

## batan (v-batan)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-t-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to give birth, to breed (of animals)
    **MT**: Wellet l-annimali jew żviluppat idea f'moħħha sa milli tiġi ppubblikata.
  - **EN**: to conceive (an idea)
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to conceive.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-kelba batanet erba' ġriewi fir-razzett.
    EN: The dog gave birth to four puppies on the farm.

---

## batar (v-batar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-t-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to dock (to cut off a section of an animal's tail)
    **MT**: Qassart jew qtajt parti mid-denb ta' annimal jew għamilt bilanċ mal-piż.
  - **EN**: to counterbalance (to apply weight in order to balance)
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to counterbalance.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Batar id-denb tal-kelb minħabba r-regoli veterinarji antiki.
    EN: He docked the dog's tail due to old veterinary rules.

---

## bati (adj-bati)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-t-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: light, slight, slow
    **MT**: Li jinsab dgħajjef f'saħħa, mod jew veloċità bil-mod fl-operat.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien sate bati li kellu bżonn kura medika immedjata.
    EN: He was in a slight, slow health condition needing immediate care.

---

## batta (v-batta)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-t-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to abate, subside
    **MT**: Naqqas u kkalma l-forza jew il-pass ta meħtieġ ta' maltempita jew uġigħ.
  - **EN**: to knock, beat
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to knock, beat.
  - **EN**: to knock over
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to knock over.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ir-riħ batta matul il-lejl u l-baħar ikkalma.
    EN: The wind abated during the night and the sea calmed down.

---

## battal (adj-battal)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-t-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: vacant (house, position etc.)
    **MT**: Mhux okkupat jew li m'għandux oġġetti u nies fih, vojt.
  - **EN**: empty
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' empty.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-dar kienet battala għal aktar minn sena.
    EN: The house was vacant for more than a year.

---

## battal (v-battal)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-t-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to empty
    **MT**: Għamilt oġġett jew bini vojt billi neħħejt l-oġġetti jew il-kontenut kollu.
  - **EN**: to vacate
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to vacate.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Battal il-kaxxa mill-kotba l-antiki u naddafha.
    EN: He emptied the box of old books and cleaned it.

---

## battam (v-battam)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-t-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to plaster the walls
    **MT**: Ksejt il-ħitan b'saff ta' tikħil jew siment biex tillixxahom.
- **Alternative Forms**: bbattma
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Battam il-ħitan tal-kamra qabel ma pitter.
    EN: He plastered the room walls before painting.

---

## baxar (v-baxar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-x-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to announce, to bring news (usually of good news)
    **MT**: Ħabart jew ġabt aħbar mill-isbaħ lil xi ħadd fil-pront.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baxar l-aħbar it-tajba lill-familja kollha.
    EN: He announced the good news to the whole family.

---

## baxx (adj-baxx)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: low, down
    **MT**: Mhux għoli fil-livell, fl-għoli jew fil-volum tal-ħoss.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Jinsab f'livell baxx ta meħtieġ fit-triq.
    EN: It is located at a low level on the road.

---

## baxxa (adj-baxxa)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-x-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: feminine singular of baxx
    **MT**: Forma femminili ta' baxx, li tinsab b'dimensjoni jew għoli baxx.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tabella kienet mwaħħla f'pożizzjoni baxxa.
    EN: The sign was attached in a low position.

---

## baxxa (v-baxxa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-x-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to lower
    **MT**: Niżżilt il-livell, il-volum jew l-għoli ta' oġġett jew deċiżjoni.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baxxa l-volum tar-radju biex ma jiddisturbax lir-residenti.
    EN: He lowered the radio volume so as not to disturb residents.

---

## baxxar (n-baxxar)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-x-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: agent noun of baxar and baxxar: announcer, bringer of good news
    **MT**: Persuna li ġġib aħbarijiet tajbin u mportanti, mellaħ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baxxar ġab l-aħbar li l-proġett kien approvat.
    EN: The announcer brought news that the project was approved.

---

## baxxar (v-baxxar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-x-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: synonym of baxar
    **MT**: Baxar u ħabart aħbar it-tajba uffiċjalment.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baxxar ir-riżultati pożittivi fil-gazzetta.
    EN: He announced the positive results in the newspaper.

---

## baxà (n-baxà)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: pasha
    **MT**: Titlu u rwol ta' uffiċjal jew gvernatur ta' livell għoli fl-Imperu Ottoman.
  - **EN**: one who is often easily pleased by others
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal one who is often easily pleased by others.
- **Alternative Forms**: paxà
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baxà laqa' l-ambaxxaturi kollha fil-palazz tiegħu.
    EN: The pasha welcomed all ambassadors to his palace.

---

## bazzika (n-bazzika)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a Maltese variant of bezique
    **MT**: Logħba tal-karti tradizzjonali li tintlagħab b'żewġ gverta kardi (bezique).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienu jilagħbu l-bazzika fil-każin kull filgħaxija.
    EN: They used to play bezique in the club every evening.

## baċellerat (n-baċellerat)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bachelor 's degree
    **MT**: Grad akkademiku tal-ewwel livell mogħti minn università wara tliet jew erba' snin studju.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kiseb il-baċellerat fl-inġinerija b'riżultat eċċellenti.
    EN: He obtained his bachelor's degree in engineering with an excellent result.

---

## baċellier (n-baċellier)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bachelor (person who has achieved bachelor's degree)
    **MT**: Persuna li ggradwat u kisbet grad ta' baċellerat f'qasam ta' studju.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baċellier il-ġdid beda xogħlu fil-kumpanija.
    EN: The new graduate started his work in the company.

## baċċan (v-baċċan)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-ċ-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to fatten
    **MT**: Tajt ikel u għamilt lil persuna jew annimal ikollu piż akbar u fula.
- **Alternative Forms**: beċċen
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baċċan il-bhejjem qabel ma ħadhom lejn is-suq.
    EN: He fattened the livestock before taking them to market.

---

## baċċaċ (v-baċċaċ)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-ċ-ċ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make chubby
    **MT**: Għamilt lil xi ħadd b'wiċċ mimli u fula f'forma ta' tarbija.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tifel baċċaċ rari matul dawn l-aħħar xhur.
    EN: The child grew chubby during these recent months.

## baġit (n-baġit)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: budget
    **MT**: Stima u pjan finanzjarju ta meħtieġ ta' dħul u spejjeż għal perjodu stabbilit.
- **Alternative Forms**: budget
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-gvern ippreżenta l-baġit nazzjonali fil-parlament.
    EN: The government presented the national budget in parliament.

## Baħamas (n-baħamas)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Bahamas (an archipelago and country in the Caribbean)
    **MT**: Pajjiż fil-Karibew magħmul minn arċipelagu ta' gżejjer.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Nassau hija l-kapitali tal-Baħamas.
    EN: Nassau is the capital of the Bahamas.

## baħar (n-baħar)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-ħ-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sea
    **MT**: Parti kbira ta' ilma mielaħ li tkopri l-akbar porzjon tal-pjaneta Art.
  - **EN**: ocean
    **MT**: Kwantità jew ammont enormi ta' affarijiet fil-ħajja.
  - **EN**: abundance, big quantity
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal abundance, big quantity.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baħar kien kalm u blu matul is-sajf kollu.
    EN: The sea was calm and blue throughout the summer.

## Baħar Adrijatiku (n-baħar-adrijatiku)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Adriatic Sea

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għaddew mill-Baħar Adrijatiku fuq il-vapur uffiċjali.

## Baħar Jonju (n-baħar-jonju)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: the Ionian Sea
    **MT**: Parti mill-Baħar Mediterran fit-Nofsinhar tal-Adrijatiku bejn l-Italja u l-Greċja.
- **Usage Examples**:

  - **EN**: Caribbean Sea
    **MT**: Baħar fit-tramuntana tal-Amerika t'Isfel bil-gżejjer tal-Karibew.

  - **EN**: Red Sea

- **POS**: noun

  - MT: Il-vapur tal-merkanzija saq permezz tal-Baħar l-Isfar.
    EN: The cargo ship sailed through the Yellow Sea.

- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-ħ-t
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Għamilt allegazzjonijiet jew kalunnja falza kontra xi ħadd biex tnaqqaslu dinjitu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baħat u saħaq dwar ir-reputazzjoni tal-ġar tiegħu minħabba għira.
    EN: He slandered his neighbour's reputation out of envy.

## baħbaħ (v-baħbaħ)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-ħ-b-ħ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to wash, to rinse

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baħbaħ il-ħwejjeġ fl-ilma nadif wara l-ħasla.
    EN: He rinsed the clothes in clean water after washing.

## baħbuħ (n-baħbuħ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: cowries
    **MT**: Qoxra żgħira tal-mollusk tal-baħar użata minn dejjem bħala flus jew dekorazzjoni (cowrie).
  - **EN**: hail fellow well met
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal hail fellow well met.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Sab baħbuħ mill-isbaħ fuq ir-ramel matul il-mixja.
    EN: He found a wonderful cowrie on the sand during the walk.

## baħnan (adj-baħnan)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-ħ-n-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: simple-minded, foolish, unwise
    **MT**: Persuna jew aġir bla moħħ, stramb u bla esperjenza jew intelliġenza.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien aġir baħnan li rriżulta f'telf ta' flus.
    EN: It was a foolish action that resulted in money loss.

---

## baħnanata (n-baħnanata)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-ħ-n-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: foolish action, silly act
    **MT**: Azzjoni silly, stupidali jew bla sens magħmula minn persuna.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rranġaw it-tnejn wara dik il-baħnanata li kienet saret.
    EN: Both made up after that silly act that was committed.

## baħnunija (n-baħnunija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-ħ-n-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: synonym of baħnanata
    **MT**: Aġir jew azzjoni stramba u bla moħħ, baħnanata.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baħnunija tiegħu xejnet l-isforz kollu tat-tim.
    EN: His foolishness ruined the whole team's effort.

## Baħrejn (n-baħrejn)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Bahrain (an archipelago, island, and country in West Asia in the Persian Gulf)
    **MT**: Pajjiż magħmul minn arċipelagu żgħir fil-Golf Persiku fl-Asja tal-Punent.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Manama hija l-kapitali ta' Baħrejn.
    EN: Manama is the capital of Bahrain.

## baħri (adj-baħri)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-ħ-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: maritime
    **MT**: Li għandu x'jaqsam mar-navigazzjoni, il-vapuri u l-baħar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ir-reġjun kellu tradizzjoni baħrija antika ħafna.
    EN: The region had a very ancient maritime tradition.

## baħri (n-baħri)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-ħ-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sailor
    **MT**: Persuna kwalifikata u mħarrġa li taħdem u timxi fuq bastimenti fil-baħar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baħri saq id-dgħajsa permezz tal-mewġ qawwi.
    EN: The sailor steered the boat through the heavy waves.

## baħħ (intj-baħħ)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: there it went!
    **MT**: Esclamazzjoni użata biex tindika li post sar vojt u li m'għad hemm xejn.
  - **EN**: all gone !
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala all gone !
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għajjat 'baħħ!' hekk kif ra l-kaxxa vojta sħiħa.
    EN: He shouted 'all gone!' as he saw the completely empty box.

---

## baħħ (n-baħħ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: void, emptiness, a state of being deserted
    **MT**: Stat ta' vojt sħiħ, post abbandunat fejn ma hemm xejn u ħadd.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm baħħ fil-bini wara t-tluq tal-ħaddiema.
    EN: There was a void in the building after the workers left.

---

## baħħar (n-baħħar)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-ħ-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sailor
    **MT**: Persuna li taħdem fil-baħar u tħaddem il-vapuri, baħri.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baħħar għadda xhur fuq l-oċean f'bastiment tal-merkanzija.
    EN: The sailor spent months on the ocean in a cargo ship.

---

## baħħar (v-baħħar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-ħ-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to sail
    **MT**: Saqt u navigated vapur jew dgħajsa fil-baħar miftuħ.
  - **EN**: to fumigate
    **MT**: Naddaft u fumigajt kamra minn mard b'duħħan u sustanzi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baħħar permezz tal-oċean u wasal b'sigurtà fil-port.
    EN: He sailed across the ocean and arrived safely in port.

---

## baħħara (n-baħħara)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-ħ-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: female equivalent of baħħar
    **MT**: Plurali ta' baħħar jew forma femminili ta meħtieġ, il-marinar u l-ekwipaġġ tal-vapur.
  - **EN**: plural of baħħar
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal plural of baħħar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baħħara kollha kienu lesti għat-tluq mill-port.
    EN: All the sailors were ready for departure from the port.

## baża’ (v-baża)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to fear, to be afraid
    **MT**: Ħsejt biża' jew kien għandek biża' kbir dwar periklu.
  - **EN**: to care for [with għal]
    **MT**: Għożżejt u ħadt ħsieb b'kura kbira lil xi ħadd jew oġġett.
- **Alternative Forms**: beża'
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baża' li jitlef ix-xogħol minħabba l-kriżi.
    EN: He feared losing his job due to the crisis.

---

## bażi (n-bażi)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: base
    **MT**: Il-parti fundamentali u s-sors ta' pedament jew deċiżjoni, basi.
  - **EN**: basis
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal basis.
  - **EN**: base
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal base.
- **Alternative Forms**: basi
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Stabbilixxa bażi b'saħħitha għar-riċerka xjentifika tiegħu.
    EN: He established a strong basis for his scientific research.

---

## bażikament (adv-bażikament)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: basically
    **MT**: Fl-aktar mudell elementari u fundamentali u fl-essenza sħiħa.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bażikament is-sistema taħdem b'żewġ buttuni prinċipali.
    EN: Basically the system works with two main buttons.

---

## bażiku (adj-bażiku)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: basic
    **MT**: Li jifforma r-reżultati fundamentali u elementari ta' sistema.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Tgħallem il-prinċipji bażiċi tal-matematika fil-klassi.
    EN: He learned the basic principles of mathematics in class.

---

## bażlokk (n-bażlokk)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bigot
    **MT**: Persuna arroganti bil-twemmin reliġjuż li tara ruħha aħjar mill-oħrajn, bażokk.
- **Alternative Forms**: bażokk
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Aġixxa bħala bażlokk u ma aċċetta l-ebda perspettiva oħra.
    EN: He acted as a bigot and accepted no other perspective.

---

## bażokk (n-bażokk)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bigot
    **MT**: Persuna b'fanatiżmu jew smugness fil-fehmiet morali tagħha.
- **Alternative Forms**: bażlokk
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għajru bażokk għaliex kien jikkritika l-għażliet tal-oħrajn.
    EN: They called him a bigot because he criticized others' choices.

---

## bażokka (n-bażokka)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bigot
    **MT**: Forma femminili ta' bażokk, mara b'fanatiżmu u mġiba smuga.
  - **EN**: female equivalent of bażokk: female bigot
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal female equivalent of bażokk: female bigot.
- **Alternative Forms**: bażokk
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Hija bażokka li ma tatollerax fehmiet differenti.
    EN: She is a female bigot who does not tolerate different views.

---

## bażokkerija (n-bażokkerija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bigotry
    **MT**: Aġir u stat ta meħtieġ ta' fanatiżmu u smugness soċjali.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-attitudni tiegħu kienet mimlija bażokkerija u preġudizzju.
    EN: His attitude was filled with bigotry and prejudice.

---

## bażuffjata (n-bażuffjata)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: clownish jest
    **MT**: Ċajta u azzjoni farsa u ridikola magħmula f'teatru jew f'pubbliku.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għamlu bażuffjata sħiħa fuq il-palk u kulħadd daħak.
    EN: They performed a clownish jest on stage and everyone laughed.

---

## bażwa (n-bażwa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-ż-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: hernia
    **MT**: Marda fejn organu jew tessut joħroġ minn toqba fil-ħajt muskolari (ernea).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien operat minħabba bażwa li żviluppat fl-istonku.
    EN: He was operated on due to a hernia that developed in the stomach.

## bażwar (v-bażwar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-ż-w-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to cause hernia
    **MT**: Għamilt xogħol b'mod traskurat u f'konfużjoni mingħajr bżonn ta meħtieġ, barważ.
  - **EN**: to perform clumsy work
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to perform clumsy work.
  - **EN**: to talk confusedly
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to talk confusedly.
  - **EN**: to discombobulate
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to discombobulate.
- **Alternative Forms**: barważ
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bażwar ix-xogħol kollu u kellu jibda mill-ġdid.
    EN: He performed clumsy work and had to start over.

---

## bażwi (adj-bażwi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-ż-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: hernial
    **MT**: Li għandu x'jaqsam jew jaffettwa mill-bażwa jew nieqes mill-enerġija.
  - **EN**: suffering from hernia
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' suffering from hernia.
  - **EN**: unenergetic
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' unenergetic.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ir-raġel bażwi kien jeħtieġ mistrieħ kontinwu.
    EN: The unenergetic man needed continuous rest.

---

## bażża’ (v-bażża)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to frighten, to scare
    **MT**: Għamilt lil xi ħadd imbeżża' jew bdejt tbeżża' l-oħrajn minn periklu.
- **Alternative Forms**: beżża'
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bażża' l-inċident lit-tfal iż-żgħar b'ħoss qawwi.
    EN: He frightened the young children with a loud noise.

## bbada (v-bbada)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Wrejt eżitazzjoni u nuqqas ta' rieda li tagħmel azzjoni minnufih.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bbada qabel ma aċċetta l-posta l-ġdida.
    EN: He was reluctant before accepting the new post.

## bbaffja (v-bbaffja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Lixxajt u pollejt wiċċ ta' metall biex jingħata lewn jilma (buff).

  - MT: Bbaffja l-karozza sakemm bdiet tilma bħal ġdida.
    EN: He buffed the car until it started shining like new.

## bbajja (v-bbajja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to mock, to wrong (someone)
    **MT**: Għamilt żufjett u baja dwar persuna oħra, baja.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bbajja lill-persuna quddiem sħabu.
    EN: He mocked the person in front of his friends.

## bbajndja (v-bbajndja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to bind (a book)
    **MT**: Legajt u għaqqadt l-irqieq ta meħtieġ ta' ktieb f'qoxra waħda uffiċjali.

## bbakkja (v-bbakkja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to back, to support
    **MT**: Appoġġjajt u tajt appoġġ morali jew finanzjarju lil xi ħadd, appoġġja.
- **Alternative Forms**: bbekkja

  - MT: Bbakkja l-kandidat tiegħu matul l-elezzjonijiet.
    EN: He backed his candidate during the elections.

---

## bbalija (v-bbalija)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to oscilate
    **MT**: Tbandalt u ċċaqlaqt b'mod mhux sod, balja.
  - **EN**: to be unsteady
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to be unsteady.
- **Alternative Forms**: balja
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bbalija fid-deċiżjoni tiegħu minħabba d-dubji.
    EN: He oscillated in his decision due to doubts.

## bbalka (v-bbalka)
- **POS**: verb

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bbalka l-vapur hekk kif ir-riħ ikkalma.
    EN: The ship settled as the wind abated.

## bballastra (v-bballastra)
- **POS**: verb

  - **EN**: to forecast the weather by observation
    **MT**: Mbassar u ppreveda t-temp billi ħarsat lejn il-mewġ u s-sħab.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bballastra li s-sajf kien se jkun sħun ħafna.
    EN: He forecasted the weather would be very hot in summer.

## bballottra (v-bballottra)
- **POS**: verb

  - **EN**: to restrain from playing a better card for a later strategy (Is there an English equivalent to this definition?)
    **MT**: Zammejt karta b'saħħitha fir-riżerva matul logħba tal-karti fil-każin.

    EN: He held back his strong card for the end of the game.

## bbalzma (v-bbalzma)
- **POS**: verb

  - MT: Bbalzmaw il-ġisem tal-farfett biex jikkonservawh fil-mużew.
    EN: They embalmed the butterfly's body to preserve it in the museum.

- **POS**: verb

    **MT**: Aġixxejt b'mod childish u traddajt lil xi ħadd bħala tarbija.
  - **EN**: to treat someone as a child
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to treat someone as a child.

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bbamboċċa matul il-laqgħa sħiħa u kulħadd rrabja.

    EN: He loafed around the whole village and didn't want to work.

  - MT: Bbankettaw flimkien matul iċ-ċerimonja tat-tieġ.

  - **EN**: to put into a bank
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to put into a bank.

  - MT: Bbankja l-kapital tiegħu fil-bank nazzjonali.

## bboxxla (v-bboxxla)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to give or indicate the direction according to a compass (Is there an English equivalent to this definition?)
    **MT**: Wrejt jew rranġajt id-direzzjoni skont il-boxxla u l-gwida.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bboxxla d-dgħajsa lejn il-Lvant fl-oċean.
    EN: He set the boat's direction according to the compass to the East in the ocean.

## bbraxxja (v-bbraxxja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Naddaft jew lixxajt id-drapp bil-xkupa żgħira jew braxx.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bbraxxja l-kowt tal-suf qabel ma ħareġ.
    EN: He brushed the woollen coat before going out.

## bbrazzja (v-bbrazzja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Zammejt u rbit partijiet ta' metall permezz ta' brazzi solidi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bbrazzja l-istruttura tal-ħadid mal-ħajt.
    EN: He braced the iron structure to the wall.

## bbrejkja (v-bbrejkja)
- **POS**: verb

    **MT**: Għafas il-brejkijiet u naqqas il-veloċità jew waqqaft il-vettura.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bbrejkja fit-tmiem tat-triq b'mod immedjat.
    EN: He braked at the end of the road immediately.

## bbrilla (v-bbrilla)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Lmejt u wrett talent jew sbuħija eċċezzjonali ma' persuni oħra.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bbrilla fir-rwol ewlieni matul il-prestazzjoni tat-teatru.
    EN: He shone in the main role during the theatre performance.

## bbrinda (v-bbrinda)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Għollejt it-tazza u esprimejt xewqa u tislima solenni flimkien max-xorb.

- **Usage Examples**:

    EN: They toasted together for the couple's health and success.

---

## bbrindja (v-bbrindja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make a toast
    **MT**: Esprimejt tislima bit-tazzi xorb, bbrinda.
- **Alternative Forms**: bbrinda
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bbrindjaw mal-klijenti fl-aħħar tal-ikla.
    EN: They toasted with the clients at the end of the meal.

- **POS**: verb

    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to interrupt.
  - **EN**: to discern
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to discern.

  - **EN**: to brood, nourish (a feeling)

  - **EN**: to build a grudge against someone
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to build a grudge against someone.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għasfur bejjet fil-friegħi tas-siġra tal-ballut.

## belgħa (n-belgħa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-l-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sip
    **MT**: L-ammont ta' likwidu meħud fl-istonku b'pass wieħed mill-ħalq.
  - **EN**: swig
    **MT**: Żarbun tad-dar artab u komdu li jintlibes mal-pedament.
  - **EN**: gulp
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal gulp.
  - **EN**: swallowing
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal swallowing.
  - **EN**: slipper
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal slipper.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħa belgħa ilma sħun qabel ma beda jitkellem.
    EN: He took a sip of warm water before he started speaking.

## belgħen (v-belgħen)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-għ-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to froth at the mouth
    **MT**: Ħarġet fowm u arja bajda mill-ħalq minħabba marda jew rabja.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-kelb marid beda jbelgħen fil-bitħa.
    EN: The sick dog started frothing at the mouth in the yard.

## belgħun (n-belgħun)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-l-għ-n

  - MT: It-tabib tah mediċina biex inaqqas il-belgħun fl-imsaren.

- **Root**: b-l-h

  - **EN**: foolish, imbecilic
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' foolish, imbecilic.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienet diskussjoni belhienija li ma waslet għal ebda riżultat.

- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-għ

    **MT**: Għamilt jew għent lil xi ħadd jibla', balla'.

    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to make someone believe anything.
- **Alternative Forms**: balla’
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bella' l-mediċina lill-pazjent b'attenzjoni.

- **Root**: b-l-h

## bellaq (v-bellaq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-q

- **POS**: conjunction

    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala even though.
- **Alternative Forms**: billi

- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: melon, melons
    **MT**: Frott kbir u ħelu b'enormità ta' ilma u qoxra iebsa fis-sajf.
  - **EN**: testicles of a donkey
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal testicles of a donkey.
- **Alternative Forms**: bettiħ
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kalu l-bettieħ b'sodisfazzjon f'jum sħun fis-sajf.
    EN: They ate the melon with satisfaction on a hot summer day.

## bews (n-bews)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-w-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of bies: several kisses
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' bies, l-att u l-azzjoni li tippreżenta bewsa u tislima bix-xufftejn.
  - **EN**: a period of kissing
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal a period of kissing.
  - **EN**: the act of kissing as such
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal the act of kissing as such.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm bews u bakkjata kbira malli waslu l-mistednin.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-w-s

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ta bewsa lil ommu qabel ma ħareġ mid-dar.

- **POS**: noun

    EN: His action was considered a Judas kiss by his colleagues.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-x-k-l
- **Definitions**:

- **Root**: b-x-x

  - MT: Il-bexx tal-uċuħ sar filgħodu kmieni.

- **POS**: verb

## bexxiexa (n-bexxiexa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine

    **MT**: Frieħ żgħir tal-ħamiema li għadu l-ponta tal-bejta.
  - **EN**: darling, honey
    **MT**: Kelma ta meħtieġ ta' imħabba mogħtija lil tifel jew tarbija.
  - **EN**: simpleton, gull
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal simpleton, gull.

- **Root**: b-ż-għ
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Ħsejt biża' jew emozzjoni ta' ansjetà kontra periklu, baża'.
  - **EN**: to care for [with għal]
    **MT**: Għożżejt u ħadt ħsieb oġġett jew persuna billi użajt kura sod.
- **Alternative Forms**: baża’
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Beża' mit-theddida u mar jirrapporta lill-pulizija.
    EN: He feared the threat and went to report to the police.

- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-ż-q
- **Definitions**:
