    **MT**: Ħriġt u tfajt il-bżieq mill-ħalq u minn ġo fih.
  - **EN**: to throw off violently
    **MT**: Semmejt u żvelajt ksur jew sigriet b'mod mhux intenzjonat.
  - **EN**: to shed (of trees)
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to shed.
  - **EN**: to reveal a secret
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to reveal a secret.
  - **EN**: to be the spitting image of
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to be the spitting image of.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Beżaq fuq l-art wara li dduq it-togħma morra.
    EN: He spat on the ground after tasting the bitter flavor.

- **Root**: b-ż-għ

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħa beżgħa kbira meta sema' l-ħabta fit-triq.
    EN: He got a big fright when he heard the crash in the street.

- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-ż-għ

## beżlaq (v-beżlaq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to suckle
    **MT**: Għamilt ħoss fix-xufftejn waqt li tixrob jew daqqajt subgħajk u beżaq fit-taħdit.
  - **EN**: to smack one's lips while drinking (Is there an English equivalent to this definition?)
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to smack one's lips while drinking.
  - **EN**: to cook badly
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to cook badly.
  - **EN**: to spit while talking
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to spit while talking.
- **Alternative Forms**: beżlek
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Beda jbeżlaq fit-taħdit tiegħu quddiem kulħadd.
    EN: He started smacking his lips and spitting while talking before everyone.

## beżlek (v-beżlek)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-ż-l-k
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to suckle
    **MT**: Daqqajt xufftejk matul ix-xorb, beżlaq.
  - **EN**: to smack one's lips while drinking (Is there an English equivalent to this definition?)
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to smack one's lips while drinking.
  - **EN**: to cook badly
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to cook badly.
  - **EN**: to spit while talking
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to spit while talking.
- **Alternative Forms**: beżlaq
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Beżlek waqt li kien qed jixrob ix-xorb sħun.
    EN: He smacked his lips while drinking the hot beverage.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-ż-q

  - **EN**: small quantity (of liquids)
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal small quantity.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħalla beżqa żgħira fil-karta tat-tessut.

## beżża' (v-beżża)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-ż-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to frighten, to scare
    **MT**: Għamilt lil xi ħadd iħoss il-biża' bil-kliem jew l-azzjonijiet tiegħek, bażża'.
- **Alternative Forms**: bażża’
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Beżża' l-qtates meta għajjat b'saħħa u marru jagħmlu r-rotta.

  - MT: Beda jbeżżaq fit-triq b'imġiba mhux xierqa.
    EN: He began spitting repeatedly in the street inappropriately.

- **Root**: b-ż-l

    EN: He was a cowardly soldier who fled from battle.

## beżżiegħi (adj-beżżiegħi)

- **Root**: b-ż-għ

## bhima (n-bhima)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-h-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a beast
    **MT**: Annimal pedali u ċerimonjali fit-trobbija tar-razzett jew selvaġġ.
  - **EN**: an animal, usually either as livestock or as a wild and/or dangerous creature
    **MT**: Persuna crudele u mingħajr sentimenti lejn nies oħra.
  - **EN**: brute, hard-hearted person
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal brute, hard-hearted person.
- **Usage Examples**:

## bi (prep-bi)
- **POS**: preposition
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: used before a consonant cluster
    **MT**: Forma tal-prepożizzjoni b' użata qabel konsonanti darbtejn, ma', permezz ta'.
- **Alternative Forms**: b
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kiteb id-dokument bi strumenti professjonali.
    EN: He wrote the document with professional instruments.

## Bibbja (n-bibbja)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: the Bible
    **MT**: Il-Ktieb Is-Santu ta' skrizzjonijiet kkonsagrati mir-reliġjon Kristjana.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Qraw silta mill-Bibbja matul iċ-ċerimonja tal-magħmudija.
    EN: They read a passage from the Bible during the baptism ceremony.

## bicycle (n-bicycle)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:

- **Alternative Forms**: bajsikil

  - MT: Raqab il-bicycle u mar bih l-iskola.
    EN: He rode the bicycle and went to school with it.

## bidded (v-bidded)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-d-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to pour out, to spill
    **MT**: Tfajt jew xext likwidu minn reċipjent b'tali mod li jinxtered dwar l-art.
  - **EN**: to overflow
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to overflow.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bidded l-ilma fit-triq meta kien iġorr il-barmil.
    EN: He spilled the water on the road while carrying the bucket.

## biddel (v-biddel)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-d-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to change, to exchange
    **MT**: Għamilt tibdil fil-forma, deċiżjoni jew ilbies u stabbilejt ġdid.
  - **EN**: to change one's clothes
    **MT**: Biddilt id-direzzjoni jew il-fehma tiegħek fuq suġġett.
  - **EN**: to change one's mind
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to change one's mind.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Biddel il-ħwejjeġ tiegħu qabel ma mar għall-festa.
    EN: He changed his clothes before going to the party.

## bidel (v-bidel)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-d-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to change something
    **MT**: Għamilt skambju u biddilt oġġett ma' ieħor f'suq jew ftehim.
  - **EN**: to exchange
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to exchange.
  - **EN**: to exchange something with/for something
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to exchange something with/for something.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bidel it-tuffieħa tiegħu mal-banana ta' sieħbu.
    EN: He exchanged his apple for his friend's banana.

- **POS**: noun

## bies (n-bies)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-w-s
- **Definitions**:

  - **EN**: Eurasian hobby (Falco subbuteo)
    **MT**: Ħuta żgħira bil-ġwienaħ li ttir fuq l-ilma fil-baħar.

    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal flying gurnard.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bies jikseb veloċità enormi meta jkun nieżel fuq il-priża.

- **POS**: adverb

## biex (conj-biex)
- **POS**: conjunction
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Konġunzjoni li tfisser bil-għan u bl-intenzjoni li twettaq azzjoni.

    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala in order that.

    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala often rather expressed in English with to + infinitive.
- **Alternative Forms**: sabiex
- **Usage Examples**:

## bieżel (adj-bieżel)

## bikka (v-bikka)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-k-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make (someone) cry
    **MT**: Għamilt lil xi ħadd jbika u joħroġ id-dmiegħ bil-kliem jew l-azzjonijiet tiegħek.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bikka lit-tifel wara li neħħielu l-lita tiegħu.
    EN: He made the child cry after taking away his toy.

---

## bikkem (v-bikkem)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-k-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make mute or speechless
    **MT**: Għamilt lil xi ħadd isir bla kelma jew mut f'mument xokkanti, bikem.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-inċident bikkem lill-familja sħiħa bid-dwejjaq.
    EN: The incident rendered the entire family speechless with sadness.

---

## bikma (n-bikma)
- **POS**: noun
- **Root**: b-k-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: muteness
    **MT**: Il-kundizzjoni u l-istat ta' persuna li tkun bla kelma u muta.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm bikma sħiħa fil-kamra wara d-deċiżjoni.
    EN: There was a complete muteness in the room after the decision.

---

## bikri (adj-bikri)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-k-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: early
    **MT**: Li jseħħ f'ħin qabel il-mument mistenni fl-istaġun jew fil-ġurnata.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għamlu ħsad bikri tal-patata minħabba s-sħana.
    EN: They had an early potato harvest due to the heat.

---

## bikri (adv-bikri)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Root**: b-k-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: early
    **MT**: Kmieni fil-ħin u fl-ewwel sigħat tal-ġurnata.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Qam bikri biex jibda l-vjaġġ lejn Għawdex.
    EN: He got up early to begin the journey to Gozo.

---

## bil-geddum (adv-bil-geddum)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sulkily
    **MT**: B'mod li juri dwejjaq, dispjaċir u nuqqas ta' sodisfazzjon f'wiċċek.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Aċċetta l-inkarigu bil-geddum minħabba li ma riedx jagħmlu.
    EN: He accepted the assignment sulkily because he didn't want to do it.

---

## bil-kemm (adv-bil-kemm)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: hardly, barely
    **MT**: B'mod diffiċli u f'ammont żgħir ħafna, b'mira u b'qasir.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bil-kemm kien jagħraf il-kliem stampat fid-dlam.
    EN: He could barely recognise the printed words in the dark.

---

## bil-lajma l-lajma (adv-bil-lajma-l-lajma)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: slowly
    **MT**: B'mod attent u bil-mod il-mod mingħajr ebda għaġġla.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Mexa bil-lajma l-lajma matul it-triq imsaffija.
    EN: He walked slowly along the polished road.

---

## bil-maqlub (adv-bil-maqlub)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Root**: q-l-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: in a contrary sense
    **MT**: F'mod oppost u bil-kontra ta' kif kien mistenni jew maħsub.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ir-riżultat kien bil-maqlub ta' dak li stennew ir-riċerkaturi.
    EN: The result was in a contrary sense to what researchers expected.

---

## bil-mod (adv-bil-mod)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: slowly
    **MT**: B'pass kiesaħ u trankwill mingħajr għaġġla fil-post jew fl-imġiba.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Is-sewwieq saq bil-mod fit-triq imxarrba.
    EN: The driver drove slowly on the wet road.

---

## bil-qiegħda (adv-bil-qiegħda)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sitting
    **MT**: F'pożizzjoni seduta fuq pultruna jew bank ta' post.
  - **EN**: in a sitting position (used with verbs like qagħad, poġġa)
    **MT**: B'mod li għandu x'jaqsam ma' in a sitting position.
- **Alternative Forms**: bilqiegħda
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien joqgħod bil-qiegħda fis-salott jaqra l-gazzetta.
    EN: He used to sit in the living room reading the newspaper.

---

## bil-wieqfa (adv-bil-wieqfa)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: standing
    **MT**: F'pożizzjoni mimlija dritta fuq saqajk mingħajr ma toqgħod sedut.
  - **EN**: in a standing position (used with verbs like qam)
    **MT**: B'mod li għandu x'jaqsam ma' in a standing position.
- **Alternative Forms**: bilwieqfa
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Baqa' bil-wieqfa matul ix-xandira sħiħa tar-radju.
    EN: He remained standing throughout the entire radio broadcast.

---

## bil-ħabi (adv-bil-ħabi)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Root**: ħ-b-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: secretly
    **MT**: B'mod sigriet u mingħajr ma tħalli lin-nies l-oħra jintebħu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Daħħal il-pakkett bil-ħabi fil-kamra tiegħu.
    EN: He brought the package secretly into his room.

---

## bilanċ (n-bilanċ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: balance
    **MT**: L-istat ta' stabbiltà ugwali fejn il-piżijiet jew il-forzi jkunu f'armonija.
  - **EN**: balance
    **MT**: Id-differenza bejn id-dħul u l-ispejjeż f'kont finanzjarju.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Zamm il-bilanċ waqt li kien miexi fuq il-ħabel.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine

    **MT**: Ruta jew għodda tal-metall użata f'arloġġi biex iżżomm il-pass eżatt.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bilanċier tal-arloġġ kien jeħtieġ aġġustament żgħir.

## bilanċjali (adj-bilanċjali)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: balance
    **MT**: Li għandu x'jaqsam mal-bilanċ jew mad-dokument tal-bilanċ finanzjarju.

  - MT: Wettaq analiżi bilanċjali tar-rapporti bankarji.

    **MT**: Mekkaniżmu jew apparat użat biex jagħmel bilanċ fl-istrutturi.

## bilaterali (adj-bilaterali)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bilateral

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Firmaw ftehim bilaterali dwar il-kummerċ u l-ekonomija.

  - **EN**: lark (bird)

  - MT: Il-bilbla tinstab spiss fil-bwar miftuħa fl-għoljiet.

  - MT: L-użu ta' ċinturin tas-sigurtà huwa bilfors fil-karozza.

    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala even though.
- **Alternative Forms**: belli
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Billi kien tard, iddeċieda li jieħu t-taksis.

## blaxer (n-blaxer)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: blusher (face makeup)
    **MT**: Prodott kosmetiku tal-kulur aħmar jew roża applikat fuq il-ħaddejn.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Applikat il-blaxer biex tagħti lewn ġdid lill-wiċċ tagħha.
    EN: She applied the blusher to give a new shade to her face.

---

## blaġa (n-blaġa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a lazy person
    **MT**: Persuna nieqsa mill-enerġija u mill-impenn li tippreferi tistrieħ u ma taħdimx.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien blaġa li ma riedx jagħmel ebda għajnuna fid-dar.
    EN: He was a lazy person who didn't want to do any help at home.

---

## blekajn (n-blekajn)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: black eye
    **MT**: Marka ikħal jew sewda madwar l-għajn kkawżata minn daqqa jew ferita (black eye).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għamel blekajn wara l-inċident tal-futbol.
    EN: He got a black eye after the football incident.

---

## blieh (v-blieh)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-h
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become foolish
    **MT**: Sirt foolish, bla sens u mingħajr intelliġenza f'aġir tiegħek.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Blieh f'mġibtu hekk kif beda jixrob l-alkoħol.
    EN: He became foolish in his behaviour as he started drinking alcohol.

---

## blieq (v-blieq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become lighter in colour
    **MT**: Sirt aktar ċar fil-kulur jew misjur u rfinat f'raba'.
  - **EN**: to become mature
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to become mature.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-frott blieq fuq is-siġar fil-ġnien tal-pjazza.
    EN: The fruit ripened and lightened on the trees in the square garden.

---

## bligħ (n-bligħ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-l-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of bela’
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' bela', l-att li tbela' u tikkonsma ikel jew likwidu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bligħ tal-mediċina kien diffiċli għall-pazjent.
    EN: The swallowing of the medicine was difficult for the patient.

---

## blinka (n-blinka)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: turn signal
    **MT**: Dawl ta' indikazzjoni tal-mewġ ta' vettura użat biex jinforma dwar id-direzzjoni (blinker).
- **Alternative Forms**: blinker
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Xgħel il-blinka tal-lemin qabel ma dar fit-triq.
    EN: He turned on the right indicator before turning into the street.

---

## blinker (n-blinker)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: turn signal
    **MT**: Dawl ta meħtieġ ta' direzzjoni fil-vetturi, blinka.
- **Alternative Forms**: blinka
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-blinker kien qed jixgħel u jintefa matul it-tidwira.
    EN: The indicator was flashing on and off during the turn.

---

## blis (n-blis)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a devil, a demon
    **MT**: Demonju, spirtu ħażin u figura tal-ħażen fid-duttrina reliġjuża.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Fil-ħrafa tradizzjonali, il-blis ipprova jtentat lill-bdiewa.
    EN: In the traditional tale, the demon tried to tempt the farmers.

---

## blizz (n-blizz)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: blitz
    **MT**: Attakk jew azzjoni intensiva u mgħaġġla f'sport jew f'operazzjoni.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Wettaq blizz sħiħ kontra l-avversarji fil-partita.
    EN: He conducted a full blitz against the opponents in the match.

---

## bliċ (n-bliċ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bleach
    **MT**: Sustanza kimika użata fil-ħasil biex tneħħi t-takkli u tagħmel drapp abjad.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Uża l-bliċ biex inaddaf il-madum tal-kamra tal-banju.
    EN: He used the bleach to clean the bathroom tiles.

---

## bliċinpawder (n-bliċinpawder)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bleaching powder
    **MT**: Trab kimiku meħud biex jillustra jew inaddaf id-drappijiet u l-uċuħ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għaqqad il-bliċinpawder mill-ilma biex jipprepara t-taħlita.
    EN: He combined bleaching powder with water to prepare the mixture.

---

## blokk (n-blokk)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: block (group of buildings demarcated by streets)
    **MT**: Grupp jew kumpless ta' bini u djar limitat mit-toroq madwaru.
  - **EN**: bloc, coalition
    **MT**: Koalizzjoni u unjoni ta' pajjiżi jew partiti politiċi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Jaħdem f'blokk ta meħtieġ ta' uffiċċji fiċ-ċentru tal-belt.
    EN: He works in an office block in the city centre.

---

## blokka (n-blokka)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: block (of), lump (of)
    **MT**: Massa solidu jew biċċa kbira ta' materjal bħall-ġebel jew silġ.
- **Alternative Forms**: bloks
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Poġġa blokka silġ kbira fir-reċipjent xorb.
    EN: He placed a large block of ice in the drink container.

---

## bloks (n-bloks)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: block (of), lump (of)
    **MT**: Biċċa kbira u solida minn materjal, blokka.
- **Alternative Forms**: blokka
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Qata' bloks tal-ġebel mir-blat fil-barriera.
    EN: He cut a block of stone from the rock in the quarry.

---

## blonġun (n-blonġun)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: great crested grebe (Podiceps cristatus)
    **MT**: Għasfur tal-ilma b'ras mżejna bir-rix li jgħum fil-bwar u fis-salini (Podiceps cristatus).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-blonġun mar taħt l-ilma biex jaqbad ħuta żgħira.
    EN: The grebe went under water to catch a small fish.

---

## blonġun prim (n-blonġun-prim)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: great crested grebe (Podiceps cristatus)
    **MT**: L-akbar speċi ta' blonġun fil-gżejjer (Podiceps cristatus).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-blonġun prim ġie osservat fis-salini minn ornitologi.
    EN: The great crested grebe was observed in the salt pans by ornithologists.

---

## blonġun sekond (n-blonġun-sekond)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: black-necked grebe (Podiceps nigricollis)
    **MT**: Speċi ta' blonġun bir-rix iswed fuq għonqu li jinsab fir-rebbiegħa (Podiceps nigricollis).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-blonġun sekond jiġi f'Malta matul il-passa.
    EN: The black-necked grebe comes to Malta during migration.

---

## blonġun żgħir (n-blonġun-żgħir)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: little grebe (Tachybaptus ruficollis)
    **MT**: L-iżgħar speċi ta' blonġun b'korp tond u kannella fil-baħar (Tachybaptus ruficollis).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-blonġun żgħir huwa magħruf għar-rotazzjoni mgħaġġla tiegħu taħt l-ilma.
    EN: The little grebe is known for its fast movement underwater.

---

## blu (adj-blu)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: blue (colour/colour)
    **MT**: Li għandu l-kulur tas-sema u tal-baħar minn dawl xemxi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Is-sema kien blu u mingħajr l-ebda sħaba fis-sajf.
    EN: The sky was blue and without any cloud in summer.

---

## blugħ (n-blugħ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-l-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of bela’
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' bela', l-att li tbela' u tikkonsma ikel, bligħ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-blugħ ta' din il-pillola huwa faċli mill-ilma.
    EN: The swallowing of this pill is easy with water.

---

## bluha (n-bluha)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-l-h
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: stupidity
    **MT**: Il-kwalità jew l-att ta' aġir foolish, silly u nieqes minn sens u intelliġenza.
  - **EN**: foolishness
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal foolishness.
  - **EN**: silliness
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal silliness.
- **Alternative Forms**: bluhija
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienet bluha li ma aċċettax l-għajnuna uffiċjali tagħhom.
    EN: It was foolishness that he did not accept their official help.

---

## bluhija (n-bluhija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: stupidity
    **MT**: Stat jew aġir ta' foolishness jew sensazzjoni silly, bluha.
  - **EN**: foolishness
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal foolishness.
  - **EN**: silliness
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal silliness.
- **Alternative Forms**: bluha
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rrifjuta dik il-bluhija tiegħu wara li ntebaħ bl-iżball.
    EN: He rejected that foolishness of his after realizing the error.

---

## bnazzi (adj-bnazzi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: fine, calm
    **MT**: Li għandu temp trankwill, xemxi u mingħajr l-ebda riħ jew maltempata.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienet ġurnata bnazzija bil-baħar kalm u lixx.
    EN: It was a fine day with a calm and smooth sea.

---

## bnazzi (n-bnazzi)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: fine weather
    **MT**: Stat ta' temp mill-isbaħ u trankwill fil-pajjiż u fil-baħar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-baħħara ħadu vantaġġ mill-bnazzi biex joħorġu bid-dgħajjes.
    EN: The sailors took advantage of the fine weather to go out with boats.

---

## bnejqa (n-bnejqa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of bniqa
    **MT**: Bniqa żgħira tad-drapp mdaħħla fil-ħwejjeġ biex twessa' l-ilbies.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Zied bnejqa żgħira fit-truf tal-libsa.
    EN: He added a small gusset to the edges of the dress.

---

## bniedem (n-bniedem)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: human, human being, man (male or unspecified sex)
    **MT**: Persuna individwali jew membru tar-razza umana (raġel jew mara).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kull bniedem għandu dritt għar-rispett u s-sigurtà.
    EN: Every human being has a right to respect and security.

---

## bniedma (n-bniedma)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: female equivalent of bniedem: a female human being
    **MT**: Forma femminili ta' bniedem, mara jew tfajla meqjusa bħala membru tal-komunità umana.
  - **EN**: a girl or woman viewed as a member of the human race
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal a girl or woman viewed as a member of the human race.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Hija bniedma b'qalb tajba u onesta ma' kulħadd.
    EN: She is a female human being with a good and honest heart with everyone.

---

## bnin (adj-bnin)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-n-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: delicious
    **MT**: Li għandu togħma mill-isbaħ u li huwa nutrittiv ħafna fil-kwalità u l-użu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Sajru platt bnin u mimli togħma tradizzjonali.
    EN: They cooked a delicious and nutritious traditional dish.

---

## bniqa (n-bniqa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: gusset (small piece of cloth inserted in a garment)
    **MT**: Biċċa drapp jew qoxra mdaħħla f'ilbies biex tagħtih spazju aktar wiesa' (gusset).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħjata bniqa fil-qmis biex jagħmilha aktar komda.
    EN: She sewed a gusset in the shirt to make it more comfortable.

---

## Bodbod (n-bodbod)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Capricorn (constellation)
    **MT**: Is-sinjal ta meħtieġ ta' Kaplan fil-zodjaku u l-kostellazzjoni fil-mappa (Capricorn).
  - **EN**: Capricorn (zodiac sign)
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal Capricorn.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Is-sinjal ta' Bodbod huwa magħruf għas-sodizza u r-rażan tiegħu.
    EN: The sign of Capricorn is known for its firmness and restraint.

---

## bogħod (n-bogħod)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-għ-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: distance, being far away
    **MT**: Id-distanza u l-ispazju li jissepara post minn ieħor f'distanza kbira.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għex f'bogħod kbir mill-belt prinċipali.
    EN: He lived at a great distance from the main city.

---

## bohrju (n-bohrju)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bohrium (chemical element)
    **MT**: Element kimiku radjoattiv b'simbolu Bh u numru atomiku 107.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bohrju huwa element sintetiċi mproduċi fil-laboratorji.
    EN: Bohrium is a synthetic element produced in laboratories.

---

## bojkott (n-bojkott)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: boycott
    **MT**: L-att uffiċjali ta' nuqqas ta meħtieġ ta' xiri jew parteċipazzjoni minn komunità.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-organizzazzjoni stabbiliet bojkott kontra l-prodotti importati.
    EN: The organisation established a boycott against imported products.

---

## bokkla (n-bokkla)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: buckle
    **MT**: Għodda jew mfassla tal-metall użata biex tgħaqqad u tiffissa ċinturin jew xagħar.
  - **EN**: hairclip
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal hairclip.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għalaq ix-xagħar tagħha b'bokkla mżejjena bir-ram.
    EN: She fastened her hair with a brass-decorated clip.

---

## bokra (n-bokra)
- **POS**: noun
- **Root**: b-k-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: early morning
    **MT**: L-ewwel sigħat tal-jum madwar it-tlugħ tax-xemx, filgħodu kmieni.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħarġu mill-lukanda f'bokra l-ġurnata biex jibdew il-vjaġġ.
    EN: They left the hotel early morning to begin the trip.

---

## boks (n-boks)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: black eye
    **MT**: Marka jew ferita ikħal f'wiċċ il-bniedem madwar l-għajn (black eye).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rċieva boks madwar għajnu matul it-taqbid.
    EN: He received a black eye around his eye during the fight.

---

## bokser (n-bokser)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: boxer (participant in a boxing match)
    **MT**: Atleta professjonali li jikkompeti u jiġġieled fil-sport tal-boksing.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bokser rebaħ il-partita fl-ewwel round.
    EN: The boxer won the match in the first round.

---

## boksing (n-boksing)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: boxing
    **MT**: L-isport tal-ġlied bil-ponnijiet f'ring protett fir-regoli.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-boksing huwa sport li jeħtieġ saħħa u veloċità kbira.
    EN: Boxing is a sport requiring great strength and speed.

---

## boll (n-boll)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: postage stamp
    **MT**: Pezz żgħir paper ipprintjata magħquda ma meħtieġ f'posta uffiċjali (bolla).
  - **EN**: brand
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal brand.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Mwaħħal boll ġdid fuq il-pakkett postali qabel it-tluq.
    EN: A new postage stamp was affixed to the postal package before departure.

---

## boloq (adj-boloq)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: b-l-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: plural of iblaq
    **MT**: Plurali ta' iblaq, b'kulur imħallat abjad u iswed fl-annimali.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-klieb boloq kienu qed jilagħbu fil-bitħa.
    EN: The dusky dogs were playing in the courtyard.

---

## boloq (v-boloq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: b-l-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to grow old
    **MT**: Sirt xjaħ u kiber fl-età b'bidla fil-kulur tax-xagħar jew tal-ġilda.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Boloq b'dinjità ma' matul ix-xhur li għaddew.
    EN: He grew old with dignity over the past months.

---

## bolt (n-bolt)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bolt (metal fastener)
    **MT**: Perna jew għodda tal-metall mibruma b'kamin u użata fit-twaħħil.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għalaq il-bieb bil-bolt tal-ħadid mill-ewwel.
    EN: He fastened the door with the iron bolt immediately.

---

## bomba (n-bomba)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bomb
    **MT**: Apparat u sustanza splussiva iddisinjata biex teqred u tfaqqa'.
  - **EN**: petard
    **MT**: Avviż jew aħbar mhux mistennija u xokkanti ħafna.
  - **EN**: any big - sized round object
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal any big - sized round object.
  - **EN**: unexpected news
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal unexpected news.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bomba faqqret it-toroq matul il-gwerra.
    EN: The bomb exploded on the streets during the war.

---

## bomblu (n-bomblu)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: water pot, pitcher
    **MT**: Reċipjent tal-fuħħar bil-manku użat biex iġorr l-ilma kiesaħ fil-kċina.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Xorbu ilma frisk mill-bomblu tal-fuħħar.
    EN: They drank fresh water from the earthenware pitcher.

---

## bonasera (intj-bonasera)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: good evening
    **MT**: Tislima tradizzjonali użata filgħaxija magħmula lin-nies, il-lejl it-tajjeb!
- **Alternative Forms**: bonasira
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għajjat 'bonasera!' hekk kif daħal fis-sala tat-tieġ.
    EN: He called out 'good evening!' as he entered the wedding hall.

---

## bonasira (intj-bonasira)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: good evening
    **MT**: Tislima tradizzjonali tal-għaxija, bonasera.
- **Alternative Forms**: bonasera
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Awgura bonasira lill-mistednin kollha li kienu preżenti.
    EN: He wished good evening to all the guests present.

---

## bonn (n-bonn)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: stem
    **MT**: Iz-zokk prinċipali u l-parti t'isfel ta' siġra jew pjanta, bont.
- **Alternative Forms**: bont
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ziedu t-tikħil mal-bonn tas-siġra biex jipproteġuha.
    EN: They added mortar around the tree stem to protect it.

---

## bonswa (adj-bonswa)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: mad, crazy
    **MT**: Persuna jew aġir stramb, maħrub mill-moħħ u bizzarr, iblaq.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienbniedem bonswa li jagħmel affarijiet bla sens.
    EN: He was a crazy person who did senseless things.

---

## bonswa (intj-bonswa)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: good evening
    **MT**: Tislima tal-għaxija derivata mill-Franċiż (bonsoir) jew espressjoni dwar stat bla tama.
  - **EN**: Indicating a hopeless condition
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala Indicating a hopeless condition.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għedna 'bonswa' u rritornajna d-dar b'mod trankwill.
    EN: We said 'good evening' and returned home peacefully.

---

## bont (n-bont)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-n-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: stem
    **MT**: Iz-zokk u l-istruttura prinċipali tal-pjanta, bonn.
- **Alternative Forms**: bonn
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bont tas-siġra kien b'saħħtu u oħsu ħafna.
    EN: The stem of the tree was very strong and thick.

---

## bontà (n-bontà)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: goodness
    **MT**: Il-kwalità u l-karatteristika ta' dak li huwa tajjeb, generuż u pur fis-sentiment.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Wera bontà kbira billi għen lill-familja fil-bżonn.
    EN: He showed great goodness by helping the family in need.

## bonġu (intj-bonġu)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: good morning
    **MT**: Espressjoni ta' tislima użata filgħodu kmieni, l-għodwa t-tajba!
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Awgura 'bonġu' lill-għalliem hekk kif daħal fil-klassi.
    EN: He wished 'good morning' to the teacher as he entered the classroom.

## boq (intj-boq)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Used to express indecision or uncertainty
    **MT**: Esclamazzjoni użata biex tesprimi nuqqas ta' għarfien jew dubju, ma nafshomx!
  - **EN**: dunno
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala dunno.
- **Alternative Forms**: boqq
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għajjat 'boq!' meta staqsewh fejn kien l-oġġett.
    EN: He said 'dunno!' when they asked him where the object was.

- **POS**: noun

  - MT: Il-boqgħa kienet mimlija uċuħ tar-raba' green.

## boqni (n-boqni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Persuna b'wiċċ tond jew kelma ta' mħabba lejn kelb żgħir u artab.
  - **EN**: A term of endearment for dogs.
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal A term of endearment for dogs.

- **POS**: noun

- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: scrubbing brush
    **MT**: Xkupa żgħira b'friegħi iebsa użata biex tobrox it-tajn.
  - **EN**: scoundrel
    **MT**: Persuna mischievous jew arrogant f'imġibatha.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Uża l-broxk biex inaddaf it-tajn mill-lingi.
    EN: He used the scrubbing brush to clean mud from the boots.

## bruda (n-bruda)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-r-d
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal coolness.
  - **EN**: lukewarmness
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal lukewarmness.
  - **EN**: lack of enthusiasm
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal lack of enthusiasm.

- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sea cucumber, holothuroid (any echinoderm of the class Holothuroidea)
    **MT**: Annimal tal-baħar f'forma tonda, bożżu / sea cucumber.
- **Alternative Forms**: bożżu
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bużżu instab fil-qiegħ tar-ramel matul l-għadis.
    EN: The sea cucumber was found at the bottom of the sand during diving.

## bwajda (n-bwajda)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-j-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of bajda
    **MT**: Bajda żgħira ħafna u ċkejkna mit-tjur.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għasfur poġġa bwajda waħda fil-bejta.
    EN: The bird laid a single small egg in the nest.

## bwejba (n-bwejba)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine

  - **EN**: diminutive of bieb
    **MT**: Bieb żgħir jew toqba żgħira fid-difiża ta' bini.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Fetħu bwejba fil-ħajt biex jidħlu bla storbju.

## bwejjeb (n-bwejjeb)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of bieb
    **MT**: Plurali u forma ċkejkna ta' bieb u ftuħ fil-bini.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm bwejjeb żgħir fl-istruttura l-antika.
    EN: There was a small door in the ancient structure.

## bwejjet (n-bwejjet)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-w-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of but
    **MT**: Plurali u forma ċkejkna ta' but fil-ħwejjeġ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm bwejjet żgħar mwaħħla fil-ġakketta tiegħu.
    EN: There were small pockets attached to his jacket.

## bwejta (n-bwejta)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-w-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of but
    **MT**: But żgħir f'libsa użat għall-moniti żgħar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ppoġġa r-riċevuta fil-bwejta ta' bil-qmis.
    EN: He put the receipt in the small pocket of his shirt.

## bxara (n-bxara)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-x-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: announcement, message, especially a positive one
    **MT**: Aħbar mill-isbaħ u pożittiva ppreżentata uffiċjalment lil xi ħadd.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ġab bxara mill-isbaħ li rċevew l-approvazzjoni kollha.
    EN: He brought a wonderful announcement that they received all approval.

## bxima (n-bxima)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: placenta
    **MT**: Organu u membrana temporanja li tittrasferixxi n-nutrijenti lit-tarbija fil-ġuf (plaċenta).
- **Alternative Forms**: pxima
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tabib eżamina l-bxima wara l-ħlas tat-tarbija.
    EN: The doctor examined the placenta after the baby's birth.

## BĊE (n-bċe)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: initialism of Bank Ċentrali Ewropew (“ European Central Bank ”)
    **MT**: Inizjali u uffiċjali tal-Bank Ċentrali Ewropew (BĊE).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-BĊE jħabbar id-deċiżjonijiet dwar ir-rati tal-mgħax.
    EN: The ECB announces decisions regarding interest rates.

## bċejċa (n-bċejċa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-ċ-ċ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of biċċa
    **MT**: Biċċa żgħira u ċkejkna minn oġġett sħiħ jew drapp.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kiel bċejċa żgħira minn mal-kejk.
    EN: He ate a tiny piece of the cake.

## bċejċen (n-bċejċen)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-ċ-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of beċċun: small pigeon
    **MT**: Beċċun żgħir jew frieħ tal-ħamiema fil-bejta.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bċejċen kienu jistennew l-ikel mill-ġenituri.
    EN: The small squabs were waiting for food from the parents.

## bħajra (n-bħajra)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-ħ-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: lake
    **MT**: Parti kbir ta' ilma ħelwa u miftuħa mwaqqfa fil-pajsaġġ (lag).
  - **EN**: melon field
    **MT**: Għalqa jew raba' mħawwel bil-bettieħ jew bil-ħsam.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm bħajra mill-isbaħ mżejjena bir-ram u s-siġar.
    EN: There was a beautiful lake decorated with sand and trees.

## bħal (prep-bħal)
- **POS**: preposition
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: like
    **MT**: Prepożizzjoni użata biex tindika xebh jew paragun ma' oġġett jew persuna użata kienet.
  - **EN**: as
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala as.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien jimxi bil-mod bħal nemla matul it-triq.
    EN: He walked slowly like an ant along the road.

---

## bħala (prep-bħala)
- **POS**: preposition
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: as, as an example of
    **MT**: Prepożizzjoni użata biex tindika rwol, funzjoni jew kariga speċifika.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Jaħdem bħala għalliem fil-kulleġġ lokali.
    EN: He works as a teacher in the local college.

---

## bħalma (conj-bħalma)
- **POS**: conjunction
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: as
    **MT**: Konġunzjoni użata biex tesprimi xebh sħiħ fil-mod ta' azzjoni, kif ukoll.
  - **EN**: in the same way that
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala in the same way that.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għamel ix-xogħol bħalma kien struwit mir-regolamenti.
    EN: He did the work in the same way that he was instructed by regulations.

---

## bħit (n-bħit)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-ħ-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of baħat: slandering
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' baħat, l-att u l-proċess ta' kalunnja u allegazzjoni falza.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bħit kien ikkritikat b'mod strett mir-residenti.
    EN: The slandering was strictly criticized by residents.

---

## bħu (intj-bħu)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: used to express disgust
    **MT**: Esclamazzjoni użata biex tesprimi disprezz jew strambjar kbir (pħu).
- **Alternative Forms**: pħu
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għajjat 'bħu!' hekk kif duq il-mediċina morra.
    EN: He uttered 'ugh!' as he tasted the bitter medicine.

---

## bħur (n-bħur)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-ħ-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: incense, olibanum
    **MT**: Sustanza aromatika li tinħaraq biex tipproduċi duħħan b'riħa tajba f'ċerimonji reliġjużi (inċens).
  - **EN**: plural of baħar
    **MT**: Plurali tal-kelma baħar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħarqu l-bħur matul iċ-ċerimonja solenni fil-knisja.
    EN: They burned incense during the solemn ceremony in the church.

## bżar (n-bżar)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-ż-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: pepper (spice)
    **MT**: Ħwawar u trab b'togħma u riħa qawwija użat fit-tisjir tal-ikel.
  - **EN**: sweet pepper, bell pepper
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal sweet pepper, bell pepper.
  - **EN**: sweet pepper, bell peppers (several peppers
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal sweet pepper, bell peppers (several peppers.
  - **EN**: pepper as a species or mass)
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal pepper as a species or mass).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Zied ftit bżar mar-riċetta biex jagħtiha togħma aħjar.
    EN: He added a little pepper to the recipe to give it a better flavor.

## bżieq (n-bżieq)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-ż-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: saliva, spittle, spit
    **MT**: Likwidu mferrex mill-ġandli tal-ħalq biex jgħin fid-diġestjoni tal-ikel (saliva).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tabib ħa kampjun tal-bżieq għat-test xjentifiku.
    EN: The doctor took a saliva sample for the scientific test.

## bżigħ (n-bżigħ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: b-ż-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of beża’: fear
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' beża', l-emozzjoni tal-biża', biża' / biżgħa.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bżigħ mit-theddid huwa normali f'sitwazzjonijiet ta' periklu.
    EN: Fear of threats is normal in dangerous situations.

## bżonn (n-bżonn)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: need
    **MT**: Il-ħtieġa u l-obbligu li tinkiseb jew tintuża persuna jew oġġett essenzjali.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm bżonn ta' għajnuna uffiċjali biex il-proġett jitlesta.
    EN: There was a need for official help to finish the project.

## bżulija (n-bżulija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: b-ż-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diligence, hard work
    **MT**: Il-kwalità u l-karatteristika ta' bniedem bieżel li jaħdem b'impenn sħiħ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien magħruf għall-bżulija tiegħu x-xogħol nazzjonali.
    EN: He was known for his diligence in national work.

## B’Buġa (n-bbuġa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Birżebbuġa (a town and local council of the Southern Region, Malta)
    **MT**: Forma mqassra u informali użata għall-lokalità ta' Birżebbuġa (B’Buġa).
- **Alternative Forms**: Birżebbuġa
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Mura lejn B’Buġa biex igawdu jum fuq ir-ramel.
    EN: We went towards Birżebbuġa to enjoy a day on the sand.

---

## ibbumbardja (v-ibbumbardja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to bomb, bombard, shell
    **MT**: Sparajt bombi u kanuni f'sekwenza mgħaġġla kontra bini jew post, bbumbardja.
- **Alternative Forms**: bbumbardja
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ibbumbardjaw il-fortizza minn fuq l-ilma.
    EN: They bombarded the fortress from the water.

---

## imb (prep-imb)
- **POS**: preposition
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: with (chiefly an ingredient, means, concomitant, quality)
    **MT**: Forma tal-prepożizzjoni b' jew ma' użata qabel konsonanti darbtejn u f'esperjenza.
  - **EN**: times, (multiplied) by
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala times, by.
- **Alternative Forms**: b'
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Mxejtu imb'xulxin bil-kunsens sħiħ.
    EN: They walked with each other with full consent.

---

## imblokka (v-imblokka)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to block
    **MT**: Waqqajt l-aċċess jew il-passaġġ permezz ta' oġġett iebes, bblokka.
  - **EN**: to plug (a hole)
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to plug.
- **Alternative Forms**: bblokka
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Imblokka t-triq bil-muntanja ta' blat wara l-waqgħa.
    EN: He blocked the road with a pile of rocks after the collapse.

---

## imbolla (v-imbolla)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to stamp
    **MT**: Mmarkajt b'bolla uffiċjali jew b'timbru fuq dokument, bbolla.
  - **EN**: to brand (an animal)
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to brand.
  - **EN**: to brand, to label, to stigmatize
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to brand, to label, to stigmatize.
- **Alternative Forms**: bbolla
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Imbolla r-riċevuta uffiċjali qabel ma jagħtiha lill-klijent.
    EN: He stamped the official receipt before giving it to the client.

---

## paxà (n-paxà)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: pasha
    **MT**: Titlu u rwol ta' uffiċjal tal-Gvern fl-Imperu Ottoman, baxà.
  - **EN**: one who is often easily pleased by others
    **MT**: Ħaġa jew kunċett li jikkorrispondi għal one who is often easily pleased by others.
- **Alternative Forms**: baxà
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-paxà stabbilixxa r-regoli tal-port fil-belt.
    EN: The pasha established the port rules in the city.

---

## ppapoċċa (v-ppapoċċa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to act childishly
    **MT**: Aġixxejt bil-childishness f'kull pass, bbamboċċa.
  - **EN**: to treat someone as a child
    **MT**: Jagħmel azzjoni relatata ma' to treat someone as a child.
- **Alternative Forms**: bbamboċċa
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ppapoċċa matul ix-xogħol kollu fil-ħanut.
    EN: He acted childishly throughout all the work in the shop.

---

## pxima (n-pxima)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: placenta
    **MT**: Organu u membrana temporanja li tittrasferixxi n-nutrijenti, bxima / plaċenta.
- **Alternative Forms**: bxima
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tabib iċċekkja l-pxima wara l-ħlas uffiċjali.
    EN: The doctor checked the placenta after the official birth.

---

## pħu (intj-pħu)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: used to express disgust
    **MT**: Esclamazzjoni ta' disprezz u disgust, bħu.
- **Alternative Forms**: bħu
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għajjat 'pħu!' meta ra l-ħmieġ fit-triq.
    EN: He uttered 'ugh!' when he saw the dirt in the street.

---

## sabiex (adv-sabiex)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: so that
    **MT**: Adverbju u konġunzjoni użata biex tesprimi skop u intenzjoni fil-pass jew deċiżjoni, biex.
  - **EN**: in order that
    **MT**: B'mod li għandu x'jaqsam ma' in order that.
  - **EN**: often rather expressed in English with to + infinitive
    **MT**: B'mod li għandu x'jaqsam ma' often rather expressed in English with to + infinitive.
- **Alternative Forms**: biex
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħadem bla heda sabiex inaqqas id-dejn tiegħu.
    EN: He worked tirelessly so that he could reduce his debt.

---

## sabiex (conj-sabiex)
- **POS**: conjunction
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: so that
    **MT**: Konġunzjoni li tfisser bil-għan li, biex.
  - **EN**: in order that
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala in order that.
  - **EN**: often rather expressed in English with to + infinitive
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala often rather expressed in English with to + infinitive.
- **Alternative Forms**: biex
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Attenda r-riċerka sabiex ikun infurmat sew fl-istudji.
    EN: He attended the research in order that he would be well informed in studies.

---
